```csharp
    [Fact]
    public void NewSpaceTypesAreAppendedAfterTheExistingOnes()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "DwellingUnit", "Corridor", "Stair", "Core", "Lobby", "Service", "Mechanical", "Other", "Mixed",
                "Office", "Retail", "Mall", "Kitchen", "Dining", "OperatingTheatre", "CleanCorridor", "DirtyCorridor", "ClinicalSupport",
                "CareBedroom", "CareCommunal", "ActivityHall",
            },
            Enum.GetNames(typeof(SpaceType)));
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Examples))]
    public void ValuesHoldTheIllustrativeDefaults(string name, SpaceType spaceType, double wwr, double heating, double cooling)
    {
        PresetValues values = ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ValuesOf(spaceType);

        Assert.Equal(name, values.Name);
        Assert.Equal(spaceType, values.SpaceType);
        Assert.Equal(wwr, values.WindowToWallRatio);
        Assert.True(values.Conditioned);
        Assert.Equal(heating, values.HeatingSetpoint);
        Assert.Equal(cooling, values.CoolingSetpoint);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Examples))]
    public void PresetsAreBuiltFromTheirValues(string name, SpaceType spaceType, double wwr, double heating, double cooling)
    {
        PresetValues values = ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ValuesOf(spaceType);
        ProgramPreset preset = ExampleNonResidentialPresets.PresetOf(spaceType);

        Assert.True(values.ToPreset().IsSuccess);
        Assert.Equal(name, preset.Name);
        Assert.Equal(spaceType, preset.SpaceType);
        Assert.Equal(wwr, preset.WindowToWallRatio);
        Assert.True(preset.Program.IsConditioned);
        Assert.Equal(
            values.Loads.Select(l => (l.Type, l.Basis, l.Value, l.Schedule)).OrderBy(l => l.Type).ThenBy(l => l.Basis),
            preset.Program.Loads.Select(l => (l.Type, l.Basis, l.Value, l.Schedule)));
        Assert.Equal($"{name} Heating Setpoint", preset.Program.Thermostat!.HeatingSetpoint.Name);
        Assert.Equal($"{name} Cooling Setpoint", preset.Program.Thermostat.CoolingSetpoint.Name);
        Assert.All(preset.Program.Thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Values, v => Assert.Equal(heating, v));
        Assert.All(preset.Program.Thermostat.CoolingSetpoint.Values, v => Assert.Equal(cooling, v));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryPresetHasItsLoads()
    {
        Assert.Equal(ExpectedLoads.Keys.OrderBy(t => t), ExampleNonResidentialPresets.SpaceTypes.OrderBy(t => t));
        foreach (KeyValuePair<SpaceType, (LoadType Type, LoadBasis Basis, double Value, string Schedule, double AnnualFraction)[]> expected in ExpectedLoads)
        {
            Assert.Equal(expected.Value, Describe(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ValuesOf(expected.Key)));
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RetailSchedulesTradeLongestOnSaturday()
    {
        // The year starts on a Monday: day 5 is the first Saturday, day 6 the first Sunday; 11:00 is the eleventh hour of the day.
        IReadOnlyList<double> occupancy = ExampleNonResidentialPresets.RetailValues.Loads.Single(l => l.Type == LoadType.Occupancy).Schedule.Values;

        Assert.Equal(0.6, occupancy[11]);
        Assert.Equal(1.0, occupancy[(5 * 24) + 11]);
        Assert.Equal(0.4, occupancy[(6 * 24) + 11]);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, occupancy[(6 * 24) + 9]);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CareSchedulesAreTheSameEveryDay()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<double> bedroom = ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareBedroomValues.Loads.Single(l => l.Type == LoadType.Occupancy).Schedule.Values;

        Assert.Equal(bedroom.Take(24), bedroom.Skip(5 * 24).Take(24));
        Assert.Equal(1.0, bedroom[2]);
        Assert.Equal(0.2, bedroom[12]);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void VentilationIsGivenOnlyForTheKitchenAndTheOperatingTheatre()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { SpaceType.Kitchen, SpaceType.OperatingTheatre },
            ExampleNonResidentialPresets.SpaceTypes.Where(t => ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ValuesOf(t).Loads.Any(l => l.Type == LoadType.Ventilation)));
    }

    private static IEnumerable<(LoadType Type, LoadBasis Basis, double Value, string Schedule, double AnnualFraction)> Describe(PresetValues values) =>
        values.Loads.Select(l => (l.Type, l.Basis, l.Value, l.Schedule.Name, Math.Round(l.Schedule.Values.Sum(), 6)));
}
```

Only `SpaceType` changes: the summaries of the enum, `Core`, `Lobby`, and `Service`, and the twelve values appended after `Mixed`. `HeatingCooling`, `Thermostat`, and `ZoneProgram` are unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Schedules;

namespace Lod.Core.Programs;

/// <summary>
/// Semantic category of a space (spec §7). Rooms are never modelled (D-009); a non-residential zone is one department (D-097). Values are only appended,
/// so existing values keep their order (ADR-014).
/// </summary>
public enum SpaceType
{
    /// <summary>One dwelling unit; never subdivided.</summary>
    DwellingUnit,

    /// <summary>Circulation corridor.</summary>
    Corridor,

    /// <summary>Stair.</summary>
    Stair,

    /// <summary>Service or circulation core, such as an office service core with lifts, stairs, toilets, and risers.</summary>
    Core,

    /// <summary>Entrance lobby or public foyer.</summary>
    Lobby,

    /// <summary>Service space, such as the support rooms of a foyer: changing rooms, stores, toilets.</summary>
    Service,

    /// <summary>Mechanical space.</summary>
    Mechanical,

    /// <summary>Anything else.</summary>
    Other,

    /// <summary>A simplified zone combining several space types.</summary>
    Mixed,

    /// <summary>An office work area (ADR-014).</summary>
    Office,

    /// <summary>The shops of one mall wing, or one anchor store (ADR-014).</summary>
    Retail,

    /// <summary>The public mall route of a shopping centre (ADR-014).</summary>
    Mall,

    /// <summary>A kitchen with its servery, one food-service department (ADR-014).</summary>
    Kitchen,

    /// <summary>A dining field (ADR-014).</summary>
    Dining,

    /// <summary>A bank of operating theatres (ADR-014).</summary>
    OperatingTheatre,

    /// <summary>The clean route of an operating suite (ADR-014).</summary>
    CleanCorridor,

    /// <summary>The dirty (disposal) route of an operating suite (ADR-014).</summary>
    DirtyCorridor,

    /// <summary>The support rooms of an operating suite: scrub, anaesthesia, stores, recovery, sluice (ADR-014).</summary>
    ClinicalSupport,

    /// <summary>A group of care bedrooms of an elder-care home (ADR-014).</summary>
    CareBedroom,

    /// <summary>The communal and service hub of an elder-care home (ADR-014).</summary>
    CareCommunal,

    /// <summary>An activity hall for sport or community use (ADR-014).</summary>
    ActivityHall,
}

/// <summary>Heating and cooling setpoints of a conditioned zone.</summary>
public sealed class Thermostat
{
    private Thermostat(Schedule heatingSetpoint, Schedule coolingSetpoint)
    {
        HeatingSetpoint = heatingSetpoint;
        CoolingSetpoint = coolingSetpoint;
    }

    /// <summary>Heating setpoint, °C.</summary>
    public Schedule HeatingSetpoint { get; }

    /// <summary>Cooling setpoint, °C.</summary>
    public Schedule CoolingSetpoint { get; }

    /// <summary>Creates a thermostat.</summary>
    /// <param name="heatingSetpoint">A temperature schedule.</param>
    /// <param name="coolingSetpoint">A temperature schedule.</param>
    /// <returns>The thermostat or validation errors.</returns>
    public static Result<Thermostat> Create(Schedule heatingSetpoint, Schedule coolingSetpoint)
    {
        Diagnostic[] diagnostics = new[] { heatingSetpoint, coolingSetpoint }
            .Where(s => s.Kind != ScheduleKind.Temperature)
            .Select(s => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.ScheduleKindMismatch, "Setpoints need temperature schedules.", s.Name))
            .ToArray();
        return Result.FromDiagnostics(() => new Thermostat(heatingSetpoint, coolingSetpoint), diagnostics);
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// The energy-relevant program of a zone: its loads and, for conditioned zones, its thermostat. Conditioning comes from the program preset (D-038).
/// </summary>
public sealed class ZoneProgram
{
    private ZoneProgram(LoadDefinition[] loads, Thermostat? thermostat)
    {
        Loads = Array.AsReadOnly(loads);
        Thermostat = thermostat;
    }

    /// <summary>Loads ordered by <see cref="LoadType"/>, then <see cref="LoadBasis"/>; at most one per type and basis (D-047).</summary>
    public IReadOnlyList<LoadDefinition> Loads { get; }

    /// <summary>Setpoints of a conditioned zone; <c>null</c> for an unconditioned zone.</summary>
    public Thermostat? Thermostat { get; }

    /// <summary>Whether the zone is conditioned.</summary>
    public bool IsConditioned => Thermostat is not null;

    /// <summary>The load of a type expressed in a basis, or <c>null</c> when the program has none.</summary>
    /// <param name="type">Load type.</param>
    /// <param name="basis">Load basis.</param>
    /// <returns>The load or <c>null</c>.</returns>
    public LoadDefinition? Find(LoadType type, LoadBasis basis) => Loads.FirstOrDefault(l => l.Type == type && l.Basis == basis);

    /// <summary>
    /// Creates a program. A load type may appear once per basis, e.g. ventilation per person plus per floor area; its components add up (D-047).
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="loads">Loads; at most one per type and basis; per-person loads need an occupancy load.</param>
    /// <param name="thermostat">Setpoints for a conditioned zone, or <c>null</c> for an unconditioned zone.</param>
    /// <returns>The program or validation errors.</returns>
    public static Result<ZoneProgram> Create(IEnumerable<LoadDefinition> loads, Thermostat? thermostat)
    {
        LoadDefinition[] ordered = loads.OrderBy(l => l.Type).ThenBy(l => l.Basis).ToArray();
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        foreach (IGrouping<(LoadType Type, LoadBasis Basis), LoadDefinition> group in ordered.GroupBy(l => (l.Type, l.Basis)).Where(g => g.Count() > 1))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.DuplicateLoadType, $"Load type {group.Key.Type} {group.Key.Basis} appears {group.Count()} times."));
        }

        if (ordered.Any(l => l.Basis == LoadBasis.PerPerson) && ordered.All(l => l.Type != LoadType.Occupancy))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.PerPersonWithoutOccupancy, "Per-person loads need an occupancy load in the same program."));
        }

        return Result.FromDiagnostics(() => new ZoneProgram(ordered, thermostat), diagnostics);
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Schedules;

namespace Lod.Core.Programs;

/// <summary>
/// Illustrative non-residential presets (ADR-014, D-094): one per space type of the four-source families' departments (D-097). The values are
/// plausible round-number choices for development and tests; they are NOT taken from the DOE prototypes, any standard, or measured data (D-023).
/// Every preset is conditioned. Sourced presets are a separate research input (see docs/research/program-presets.md).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// Schedules are fractions per hour of the day; the year starts on a Monday. Monday to Friday use the weekday profile and Saturday and Sunday the
/// weekend profile, except the retail schedules, which have their own Saturday and Sunday profiles because shops trade longest on Saturday.
/// </remarks>
public static class ExampleNonResidentialPresets
{
    private const DayOfWeek FirstDayOfYear = DayOfWeek.Monday;

    private const int DaysPerWeek = 7;

    /// <summary>The space types that have a preset here, in the order of ADR-014.</summary>
    public static IReadOnlyList<SpaceType> SpaceTypes { get; } = Array.AsReadOnly(Types);

    /// <summary>The preset values of a space type.</summary>
    /// <param name="spaceType">One of <see cref="SpaceTypes"/>.</param>
    /// <returns>The values.</returns>
    /// <exception cref="ArgumentOutOfRangeException">The space type has no non-residential example preset.</exception>
    public static PresetValues ValuesOf(SpaceType spaceType) => spaceType switch
    {
        SpaceType.Office => OfficeValues,
        SpaceType.Core => CoreValues,
        SpaceType.Retail => RetailValues,
        SpaceType.Mall => MallValues,
        SpaceType.Kitchen => KitchenValues,
        SpaceType.Dining => DiningValues,
        SpaceType.OperatingTheatre => OperatingTheatreValues,
        SpaceType.CleanCorridor => CleanCorridorValues,
        SpaceType.DirtyCorridor => DirtyCorridorValues,
        SpaceType.ClinicalSupport => ClinicalSupportValues,
        SpaceType.CareBedroom => CareBedroomValues,
        SpaceType.CareCommunal => CareCommunalValues,
        SpaceType.Lobby => LobbyValues,
        SpaceType.ActivityHall => ActivityHallValues,
        SpaceType.Service => ServiceValues,
        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(spaceType), spaceType, "No non-residential example preset for this space type."),
    };

    /// <summary>The preset of a space type.</summary>
    /// <param name="spaceType">One of <see cref="SpaceTypes"/>.</param>
    /// <returns>The preset.</returns>
    /// <exception cref="ArgumentOutOfRangeException">The space type has no non-residential example preset.</exception>
    public static ProgramPreset PresetOf(SpaceType spaceType) => spaceType switch
    {
        SpaceType.Office => Office,
        SpaceType.Core => Core,
        SpaceType.Retail => Retail,
        SpaceType.Mall => Mall,
        SpaceType.Kitchen => Kitchen,
        SpaceType.Dining => Dining,
        SpaceType.OperatingTheatre => OperatingTheatre,
        SpaceType.CleanCorridor => CleanCorridor,
        SpaceType.DirtyCorridor => DirtyCorridor,
        SpaceType.ClinicalSupport => ClinicalSupport,
        SpaceType.CareBedroom => CareBedroom,
        SpaceType.CareCommunal => CareCommunal,
        SpaceType.Lobby => Lobby,
        SpaceType.ActivityHall => ActivityHall,
        SpaceType.Service => Service,
        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(spaceType), spaceType, "No non-residential example preset for this space type."),
    };

    private static PresetValues Values(string name, SpaceType spaceType, double windowToWallRatio, double heating, double cooling, params PresetLoadValue[] loads) =>
        new(name, spaceType, windowToWallRatio, Conditioned: true, heating, cooling, loads);

    private static double[] Flat(double value) => Enumerable.Repeat(value, HoursPerDay).ToArray();

    private static Schedule Constant(string name, double value) =>
        Schedule.Constant(name, ScheduleKind.Fraction, value).Value;

    private static Schedule Profile(string name, double[] weekday, double[] weekend) =>
        Schedule.FromDailyProfiles(name, ScheduleKind.Fraction, weekday, weekend, FirstDayOfYear).Value;

    /// <summary>A year of fractions with one profile for Monday to Friday, one for Saturday, and one for Sunday.</summary>
    private static Schedule Weekly(string name, double[] weekday, double[] saturday, double[] sunday)
    {
        var values = new double[Schedule.HoursPerYear];
        for (int hour = 0; hour < values.Length; hour++)
        {
            var day = (DayOfWeek)(((int)FirstDayOfYear + (hour / HoursPerDay)) % DaysPerWeek);
            double[] profile = day switch
            {
                DayOfWeek.Saturday => saturday,
                DayOfWeek.Sunday => sunday,
                _ => weekday,
            };
            values[hour] = profile[hour % HoursPerDay];
        }

        return Schedule.Create(name, ScheduleKind.Fraction, values).Value;
    }
}
```

### Task 6: Default preset components for the non-residential space types, with icons

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/PresetComponents.cs` (S4.1 file), `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Goo/BemGoo.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S4.2), `scripts/make_icons.py` (S4.1 file, last changed in S8.3)
- Create (generated): `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/OfficePresetComponent.png`, `CorePresetComponent.png`, `RetailPresetComponent.png`, `MallPresetComponent.png`, `KitchenPresetComponent.png`, `DiningPresetComponent.png`, `OperatingTheatrePresetComponent.png`, `CleanCorridorPresetComponent.png`, `DirtyCorridorPresetComponent.png`, `ClinicalSupportPresetComponent.png`, `CareBedroomPresetComponent.png`, `CareCommunalPresetComponent.png`, `LobbyPresetComponent.png`, `ActivityHallPresetComponent.png`, `ServicePresetComponent.png`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ExampleNonResidentialPresets` (Task 5); the S4.1 base `DefaultPresetComponent` (panel *1 Program*, one input per field of `PresetValues` with its default, the label and unit of every `LoadType` and `LoadBasis`, which already cover gas equipment in W/m² and ventilation in 1/h, so no mapping is extended; the remark "Illustrative values; not sourced from DOE prototypes or standards."); `PreviewGoo.SpaceTypeColour` (private); `IconLibrary.For` and the `Icons\*.png` embedding of `Lod.Grasshopper.csproj` (S4.1, unchanged).
- Produces: fifteen sealed components after *Stair Preset*, each with the GUID of the Global Constraints, the nickname Office, Core, Retail, Mall, Kitchen (Kitch), Dining (Dine), Operating Theatre (OT), Clean Corridor (Clean), Dirty Corridor (Dirty), Clinical Support (ClinS), Care Bedroom (CBed), Care Communal (CComm), Lobby, Activity Hall (Hall), and Service (Serv), and its `Defaults` from `ExampleNonResidentialPresets`; a preview colour for each of the twelve new space types (`Lobby` and `Service` keep the default colour); fifteen motifs in the Program orange of the residential presets (an office block with a grid of windows, a service core with two lift doors and an up and down arrow, a shopping bag, shop blocks along a white T of mall routes, a cooking pot, a plate between a fork and a knife, a medical cross, a corridor with a white route and one with a hatched route, shelves beside a small medical cross, a bed, a round table with four people, a glazed entrance front with an open door, a sports court with its centre line and circle, and three lockers), mapped after `StairPresetComponent`. 44 components, 6 parameters, and 51 icons.

- [x] **Step 1: Replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/PresetComponents.cs` (full file)**

The fifteen classes are added at the end; the base class and the three residential components are unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and solving of the default preset components (D-061): one component per space type whose inputs all default to its
/// <see cref="PresetValues"/>, so it works with nothing connected. Inputs: Name, WWR, Conditioned, Heating, Cooling, then a value and an optional
/// schedule per load of the defaults, in their order.
/// </summary>
public abstract class DefaultPresetComponent : GH_Component
{
    private const string IllustrativeRemark = "Illustrative values; not sourced from DOE prototypes or standards.";
    private const int FirstLoadInput = 5;

    protected DefaultPresetComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Program)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    /// <summary>The default values; read while inputs are registered, so they must not depend on instance state.</summary>
    protected abstract PresetValues Defaults { get; }

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        PresetValues defaults = Defaults;
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Name", "N", "Preset name; descriptive only (reports, provenance, messages), never used for matching.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.Name);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("WWR", "WWR", "Window-to-wall ratio in [0, 1) used by the plan generator for every exterior wall.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.WindowToWallRatio);
        pManager.AddBooleanParameter("Conditioned", "Cond", "Whether the space is conditioned (D-038); the setpoints are ignored when it is not.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.Conditioned);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Heating", "H", "Constant heating setpoint, °C.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.HeatingSetpoint);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Cooling", "C", "Constant cooling setpoint, °C.", GH_ParamAccess.item, defaults.CoolingSetpoint);
        foreach (PresetLoadValue load in defaults.Loads)
        {
            (string label, string nickname) = LoadLabel(defaults.Loads, load);
            pManager.AddNumberParameter(label, nickname, $"{label} design value, {Unit(load.Type, load.Basis)}.", GH_ParamAccess.item, load.Value);
            int schedule = pManager.AddParameter(
                new ScheduleParameter(),
                $"{label} Schedule",
                nickname + "S",
                $"Optional fraction schedule of {label}; replaces the built-in schedule '{load.Schedule.Name}' when connected.",
                GH_ParamAccess.item);
            pManager[schedule].Optional = true;
        }
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Preset", "P", "The program preset.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        AddRuntimeMessage(GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Remark, IllustrativeRemark);
        PresetValues defaults = Defaults;
        string name = string.Empty;
        double wwr = 0.0;
        bool conditioned = false;
        double heating = 0.0;
        double cooling = 0.0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref name) || !DA.GetData(1, ref wwr) || !DA.GetData(2, ref conditioned) || !DA.GetData(3, ref heating) || !DA.GetData(4, ref cooling))
        {
            return;
        }

        var loads = new PresetLoadValue[defaults.Loads.Count];
        for (int k = 0; k < loads.Length; k++)
        {
            double value = 0.0;
            ScheduleGoo? schedule = null;
            if (!DA.GetData(FirstLoadInput + (2 * k), ref value))
            {
                return;
            }

            DA.GetData(FirstLoadInput + (2 * k) + 1, ref schedule);
            loads[k] = defaults.Loads[k] with { Value = value, Schedule = schedule?.Value ?? defaults.Loads[k].Schedule };
        }

        PresetValues values = defaults with
        {
            Name = name,
            WindowToWallRatio = wwr,
            Conditioned = conditioned,
            HeatingSetpoint = heating,
            CoolingSetpoint = cooling,
            Loads = loads,
        };
        ProgramPreset? preset = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, values.ToPreset());
        if (preset is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new ProgramPresetGoo(preset));
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Input name and nickname of a load; the basis is added when the defaults hold the load type in more than one basis (D-047).</summary>
    private static (string Label, string Nickname) LoadLabel(IReadOnlyList<PresetLoadValue> loads, PresetLoadValue load)
    {
        (string label, string nickname) = load.Type switch
        {
            LoadType.Occupancy => ("Occupancy", "Occ"),
            LoadType.Lighting => ("Lighting", "Lt"),
            LoadType.ElectricEquipment => ("Equipment", "Eq"),
            LoadType.GasEquipment => ("Gas Equipment", "Gas"),
            LoadType.DomesticHotWater => ("Hot Water", "DHW"),
            LoadType.Ventilation => ("Ventilation", "Vent"),
            _ => ("Infiltration", "Inf"),
        };
        return loads.Count(l => l.Type == load.Type) > 1
            ? ($"{label} {load.Basis}", nickname + string.Concat(load.Basis.ToString().Where(char.IsUpper)))
            : (label, nickname);
    }

    /// <summary>Unit of a load value: the magnitude unit of its type (people, W, m³/h) per its basis (ADR-007).</summary>
    private static string Unit(LoadType type, LoadBasis basis)
    {
        string magnitude = type switch
        {
            LoadType.Occupancy => "people",
            LoadType.Lighting or LoadType.ElectricEquipment or LoadType.GasEquipment => "W",
            _ => "m³/h",
        };
        return basis switch
        {
            LoadBasis.PerFloorArea => $"{magnitude}/m²",
            LoadBasis.PerPerson => $"{magnitude}/person",
            LoadBasis.PerExteriorWallArea => $"{magnitude}/m² of exterior wall",
            LoadBasis.AirChangesPerHour => "1/h (air changes per hour)",
            _ => magnitude,
        };
    }
}
```

Only the twelve colours are added to `SpaceTypeColour`.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Types;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Schedules;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;
using Rhino.Display;
using Rhino.Geometry;
using Surface = Lod.Core.Model.Surface;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;

/// <summary>Grasshopper wrapper of an immutable pipeline object. Values are never modified, so duplicates share the value.</summary>
/// <typeparam name="T">Wrapped type.</typeparam>
public abstract class BemGoo<T> : GH_Goo<T>
    where T : class
{
    protected BemGoo(T value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override bool IsValid => Value is not null;

    public override string TypeDescription => $"BEMGen {TypeName}";
}

public sealed class ScheduleGoo : BemGoo<Schedule>
{
    public ScheduleGoo(Schedule value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Schedule";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new ScheduleGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Schedule {Value.Name} ({Value.Kind})";
}

public sealed class LoadGoo : BemGoo<LoadDefinition>
{
    public LoadGoo(LoadDefinition value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Load";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new LoadGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Load {Value.Type} {Value.Value} {Value.Basis}";
}

public sealed class ProgramPresetGoo : BemGoo<ProgramPreset>
{
    public ProgramPresetGoo(ProgramPreset value)
        : base(value)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Program Preset";

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new ProgramPresetGoo(Value);

    public override string ToString() => $"Preset {Value.Name} ({Value.SpaceType}, WWR {Value.WindowToWallRatio})";
}

/// <summary>
/// Wrapper with viewport preview: zones coloured by space type (a zone with several parts as one merged volume, D-071), storeys that are represented
/// but not modelled in transparent grey (D-071, D-075), windows in blue. The preview is moved by <see cref="PreviewOffset"/> (D-062); the wrapped
/// value keeps its true coordinates.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="T">Wrapped type.</typeparam>
public abstract class PreviewGoo<T> : BemGoo<T>, IGH_PreviewData
    where T : class
{
    private static readonly Color WindowColour = Color.FromArgb(70, 140, 220);
    private static readonly Color UnmodelledColour = Color.FromArgb(150, 150, 150);
    private List<(Brep Brep, DisplayMaterial Material)>? _preview;

    protected PreviewGoo(T value, Vector3d previewOffset)
        : base(value)
    {
        PreviewOffset = previewOffset;
    }

    /// <summary>Translation of the viewport preview only, in model units (D-062).</summary>
    public Vector3d PreviewOffset { get; }

    public BoundingBox ClippingBox
    {
        get
        {
            BoundingBox box = BoundingBox.Empty;
            foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in Preview)
            {
                box.Union(brep.GetBoundingBox(false));
            }

            return box;
        }
    }

    private List<(Brep Brep, DisplayMaterial Material)> Preview => _preview ??= BuildPreview();

    public void DrawViewportMeshes(GH_PreviewMeshArgs args)
    {
        foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial material) in Preview)
        {
            args.Pipeline.DrawBrepShaded(brep, material);
        }
    }

    public void DrawViewportWires(GH_PreviewWireArgs args)
    {
        foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in Preview)
        {
            args.Pipeline.DrawBrepWires(brep, args.Color);
        }
    }

    protected abstract IEnumerable<Zone> Zones { get; }

    protected abstract IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces { get; }

    /// <summary>Parts of storeys that are represented but not modelled, drawn transparent grey; none unless a building says so.</summary>
    protected virtual IEnumerable<ZonePart> UnmodelledParts => Enumerable.Empty<ZonePart>();

    private List<(Brep, DisplayMaterial)> BuildPreview()
    {
        var items = new List<(Brep, DisplayMaterial)>();
        foreach (Zone zone in Zones)
        {
            var material = new DisplayMaterial(SpaceTypeColour(zone.SpaceType), 0.6);
            IEnumerable<Brep> volumes = zone.Parts.Count > 1 ? RhinoGeometry.MergedZoneBreps(zone) : RhinoGeometry.ZoneBreps(zone);
            items.AddRange(volumes.Select(b => (b, material)));
        }

        var unmodelled = new DisplayMaterial(UnmodelledColour, 0.85);
        items.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.PartBreps(UnmodelledParts).Select(b => (b, unmodelled)));

        var glass = new DisplayMaterial(WindowColour, 0.2);
        foreach (WallSurface wall in Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>())
        {
            items.AddRange(RhinoGeometry.WindowBreps(wall).Select(b => (b, glass)));
        }

        if (!PreviewOffset.IsZero)
        {
            Transform move = Transform.Translation(PreviewOffset);
            foreach ((Brep brep, DisplayMaterial _) in items)
            {
                brep.Transform(move);
            }
        }

        return items;
    }

    private static Color SpaceTypeColour(SpaceType type) => type switch
    {
        SpaceType.DwellingUnit => Color.FromArgb(240, 200, 120),
        SpaceType.Corridor => Color.FromArgb(180, 180, 180),
        SpaceType.Stair => Color.FromArgb(200, 120, 120),
        SpaceType.Core => Color.FromArgb(160, 120, 200),
        SpaceType.Mixed => Color.FromArgb(120, 190, 160),
        SpaceType.Office => Color.FromArgb(130, 170, 220),
        SpaceType.Retail => Color.FromArgb(230, 150, 170),
        SpaceType.Mall => Color.FromArgb(210, 210, 160),
        SpaceType.Kitchen => Color.FromArgb(220, 130, 90),
        SpaceType.Dining => Color.FromArgb(240, 220, 150),
        SpaceType.OperatingTheatre => Color.FromArgb(110, 190, 200),
        SpaceType.CleanCorridor => Color.FromArgb(200, 230, 230),
        SpaceType.DirtyCorridor => Color.FromArgb(160, 140, 120),
        SpaceType.ClinicalSupport => Color.FromArgb(150, 200, 180),
        SpaceType.CareBedroom => Color.FromArgb(230, 180, 200),
        SpaceType.CareCommunal => Color.FromArgb(200, 160, 220),
        SpaceType.ActivityHall => Color.FromArgb(170, 210, 120),
        _ => Color.FromArgb(150, 170, 200),
    };
}

public sealed class PlanGoo : PreviewGoo<IGeneratedPlan>
{
    public PlanGoo(IGeneratedPlan value, Vector3d previewOffset = default)
        : base(value, previewOffset)
    {
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Plan";

    protected override IEnumerable<Zone> Zones => Value.Zones;

    protected override IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces => Value.Surfaces;

public sealed class BuildingGoo : PreviewGoo<IGeneratedBuilding>
{
    private readonly IReadOnlyList<ZonePart> _unmodelled;

    /// <param name="value">The building.</param>
    /// <param name="previewOffset">Preview Location (D-062).</param>
    /// <param name="unmodelled">Parts of the storeys the building represents but does not model, from the aggregator (preview only).</param>
    public BuildingGoo(IGeneratedBuilding value, Vector3d previewOffset = default, IReadOnlyList<ZonePart>? unmodelled = null)
        : base(value, previewOffset)
    {
        _unmodelled = unmodelled ?? new ZonePart[0];
    }

    public override string TypeName => "Building";

    protected override IEnumerable<Zone> Zones => Value.Zones;

    protected override IEnumerable<Surface> Surfaces => Value.Surfaces;

    protected override IEnumerable<ZonePart> UnmodelledParts => _unmodelled;

    public override IGH_Goo Duplicate() => new BuildingGoo(Value, PreviewOffset, _unmodelled);

    public override string ToString() => $"Building ({Value.Zones.Count} zones, {Value.Provenance.Operation})";
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Replace `scripts/make_icons.py` (full file)**

The fifteen motifs are added after `stair`, and their entries after `StairPresetComponent`; every other motif and entry is unchanged.

```python
"""Draws the BEMGen Grasshopper icons (D-063).

Every icon is drawn at 96 x 96 pixels on a transparent background and downsampled to 24 x 24 with LANCZOS, then written to
src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/<Name>.png, where <Name> is the class name of the component, parameter, or assembly info that shows it.
Each icon is a dark glyph plus the accent colour of its toolbar panel; a parameter uses the panel of the component that makes its object.
Shapes only, no fonts, so the output does not depend on installed fonts.

Run from the repository root:  python scripts/make_icons.py
Requires Python 3.10+ and Pillow. Generated with Pillow 12.3.0; the same Pillow version gives the same pixels.
The script stops with an error when an icon has no class in src/Lod.Grasshopper or a class has no icon.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / "src" / "Lod.Grasshopper"
OUTPUT = SOURCE / "Icons"

CANVAS = 96
SIZE = 24
LINE = 8  # 2 px after downsampling

DARK = (38, 38, 38, 255)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)

# One accent per toolbar panel (ComponentCategories).
INFO = (96, 125, 139, 255)
PROGRAM = (230, 140, 30, 255)
GENERATE = (46, 158, 79, 255)
SIMPLIFY = (47, 123, 216, 255)
AGGREGATE = (138, 79, 200, 255)
CONVERT = (208, 69, 58, 255)
INSPECT = (24, 150, 150, 255)

Colour = tuple[int, int, int, int]
Draw = ImageDraw.ImageDraw
Motif = Callable[[Draw, Colour], None]
Painter = Callable[[Image.Image, Colour], None]


def box(d: Draw, xy: tuple[float, float, float, float], fill=None, outline=DARK, width: int = LINE) -> None:
    d.rectangle(xy, fill=fill, outline=outline, width=width)


def line(d: Draw, points: list[tuple[float, float]], width: int = LINE, fill=DARK) -> None:
    d.line(points, fill=fill, width=width, joint="curve")


def arrow_head(d: Draw, tip: tuple[float, float], direction: str, size: float, fill=DARK) -> None:
    x, y = tip
    if direction == "right":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x - size, y + size)], fill=fill)
    elif direction == "down":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x + size, y - size)], fill=fill)


# --- motifs: each draws inside the 96 x 96 canvas -------------------------------------------------------------


def info(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.rectangle((42, 40, 54, 74), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((42, 20, 54, 32), fill=WHITE)


def assembly(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A stepped block: the building at three levels of detail.
    box(d, (8, 52, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 30, 60, 56), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 8, 36, 34), fill=accent)


def schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A 24-hour profile as bars on a time axis.
    heights = [16, 48, 72, 36, 60, 76, 24]
    for i, h in enumerate(heights):
        x = 12 + i * 12
        d.rectangle((x, 84 - h, x + 7, 83), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((4, 84, 92, 91), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((4, 4, 11, 91), fill=DARK)


def load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    bolt = [(58, 4), (18, 54), (44, 54), (34, 92), (78, 38), (52, 38), (64, 4)]
    d.polygon(bolt, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)


def preset_tag(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    tag = [(8, 30), (30, 8), (88, 8), (88, 88), (30, 88), (8, 66)]
    d.polygon(tag, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((24, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(52, 34), (76, 34)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 48), (76, 48)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 62), (76, 62)], width=6)


def house(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(48, 6), (90, 44), (6, 44)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    box(d, (16, 42, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 60, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(16, 48), (60, 48)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (78, 48), "right", 12)


def stair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    steps = [(6, 90), (6, 70), (28, 70), (28, 50), (50, 50), (50, 30), (72, 30), (72, 10), (90, 10), (90, 90)]
    d.polygon(steps, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def office(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # An office block in elevation with a grid of windows.
    box(d, (16, 6, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (18, 38, 58):
        for x in (26, 52):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 16, y + 12), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((40, 76, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def core(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A service core: a box with two lift doors and an up and down arrow.
    box(d, (8, 8, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 34, 42, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((54, 34, 76, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.polygon([(48, 12), (38, 26), (58, 26)], fill=DARK)


def retail(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A shopping bag with its handle.
    d.arc((30, 6, 66, 46), start=180, end=360, fill=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.polygon([(14, 30), (82, 30), (90, 90), (6, 90)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A branching mall in plan: shop blocks (accent) along a T of public routes (white).
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 8, 56, 88), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((8, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE)
    for a, b in [((40, 4), (40, 40)), ((56, 4), (56, 92)), ((40, 56), (40, 92)), ((4, 40), (40, 40)), ((4, 56), (40, 56))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)


def kitchen(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A cooking pot with a lid and two handles.
    box(d, (16, 40, 80, 86), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(4, 50), (16, 50)])
    line(d, [(80, 50), (92, 50)])
    d.rectangle((12, 28, 84, 38), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((42, 16, 54, 28), fill=DARK)


def dining(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A plate between a fork and a knife.
    d.ellipse((26, 22, 74, 70), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((38, 34, 62, 58), outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(12, 14), (12, 86)], width=6)
    line(d, [(84, 14), (84, 86)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((80, 14, 88, 46), fill=DARK)


def operating_theatre(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A medical cross.
    d.polygon(
        [(34, 6), (62, 6), (62, 34), (90, 34), (90, 62), (62, 62), (62, 90), (34, 90), (34, 62), (6, 62), (6, 34), (34, 34)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=LINE,
    )


def clean_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a white (clean) route and an arrow.
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((10, 42, 70, 54), fill=WHITE)
    arrow_head(d, (86, 48), "right", 12)


def dirty_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a hatched (dirty) route and an arrow.
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in range(12, 66, 14):
        line(d, [(x, 60), (x + 10, 36)], width=5)
    arrow_head(d, (86, 48), "right", 12)


def clinical_support(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Shelves of supplies beside a small medical cross.
    box(d, (6, 10, 58, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (34, 58):
        line(d, [(6, y), (58, y)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((70, 28, 82, 64), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((58, 40, 94, 52), fill=DARK)


def care_bedroom(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bed in elevation: headboard, mattress, and pillow.
    d.rectangle((6, 24, 16, 86), fill=DARK)
    box(d, (16, 54, 90, 72), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 42, 42, 54), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(86, 72), (86, 86)])


def care_communal(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A round table with four people around it.
    d.ellipse((28, 28, 68, 68), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for x, y in [(48, 12), (84, 48), (48, 84), (12, 48)]:
        d.ellipse((x - 9, y - 9, x + 9, y + 9), fill=DARK)


def lobby(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A glazed entrance front with an open door and an arrow leading in.
    box(d, (6, 10, 90, 88), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(34, 10), (34, 88)], width=4)
    line(d, [(62, 10), (62, 88)], width=4)
    d.rectangle((38, 44, 58, 88), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    arrow_head(d, (48, 84), "down", 8)


def activity_hall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A sports court in plan: the hall, its centre line, and the centre circle.
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(48, 16), (48, 80)], width=5)
    d.ellipse((34, 34, 62, 62), outline=DARK, width=5)


def service_rooms(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A row of three lockers with their handles.
    for left in (6, 36, 66):
        box(d, (left, 8, left + 24, 88), fill=accent, width=6)
        d.rectangle((left + 15, 40, left + 19, 56), fill=DARK)


def linear_generator(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Footprint with a west stair, a corridor band, and dwelling-unit divisions on both sides.
    box(d, (4, 12, 91, 83))
    d.rectangle((12, 20, 23, 75), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((28, 40, 87, 55), fill=accent)
    for x in (48, 68):
        line(d, [(x, 12), (x, 40)])
        line(d, [(x, 55), (x, 83)])


def stair_bay_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A long bar of two stair bays: dwelling (accent) | stair (dark) | dwelling, repeated, with a party wall between the bays.
    box(d, (2, 30, 93, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in (20, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 30, x + 10, 66), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(48, 30), (48, 66)], width=4)


def stair_pair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One compact plate: two dwellings (accent) around one full-depth stair (dark).
    box(d, (14, 22, 82, 74), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((42, 26, 54, 70), fill=DARK)


def gallery_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bar of dwellings (accent) between two end stairs (dark); the gallery along it is dashed, as it is not modelled.
    box(d, (4, 34, 91, 76), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 38, 18, 72), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((77, 38, 87, 72), fill=DARK)
    for x in (36, 54):
        line(d, [(x, 34), (x, 76)], width=6)
    for x in range(6, 90, 16):
        line(d, [(x, 20), (x + 9, 20)], width=6)


def terrace_row(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Attached houses in elevation: three gables sharing their side walls.
    houses = [(6, 34), (34, 62), (62, 90)]
    for left, right in houses:
        middle = (left + right) / 2
        d.polygon([(left, 46), (middle, 22), (right, 46), (right, 88), (left, 88)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for left, right in houses:
        d.rectangle((left + 10, 68, right - 10, 88), fill=DARK)


def point_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A square plate: four dwellings (accent) in a pinwheel around one central core (dark) without façades.
    box(d, (6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((36, 36, 60, 60), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((6, 36), (60, 36)), ((60, 6), (60, 60)), ((36, 60), (90, 60)), ((36, 36), (36, 90))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def winged_band(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A comb in plan: a band (bottom) with three wings projecting north (up), every wing joined to the band along its root; stairs dark.
    d.polygon(
        [(4, 90), (92, 90), (92, 8), (70, 8), (70, 62), (59, 62), (59, 8), (37, 8), (37, 62), (26, 62), (26, 8), (4, 8)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=6,
    )
    for left, right in ((4, 26), (37, 59), (70, 92)):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 30, right - 3, 38), fill=DARK)
    for x in (20, 48, 76):
        d.rectangle((x, 66, x + 6, 87), fill=DARK)


def open_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A U in plan: a north wing (top) across the full width and two side wings, around a court open to the south (bottom); stairs dark.
    d.polygon([(4, 92), (28, 92), (28, 34), (68, 34), (68, 92), (92, 92), (92, 6), (4, 6)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for x in (24, 45, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 9, x + 6, 31), fill=DARK)
    for left in (4, 68):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 58, left + 21, 66), fill=DARK)


def enclosed_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A closed ring in plan: south and north wings across the full width and west and east wings between them, around a court (the hole,
    # transparent) closed on all four sides; stairs dark.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (30, 30, 66, 66), fill=(0, 0, 0, 0), width=6)
    for y in (7, 69):
        for x in (24, 66):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 6, y + 20), fill=DARK)
    for left in (7, 69):
        d.rectangle((left, 45, left + 20, 51), fill=DARK)


def transform_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A footprint outline and the same footprint rotated and moved, joined by a turning arrow.
    box(d, (6, 52, 50, 90), width=6)
    d.polygon([(66, 12), (92, 28), (76, 56), (50, 40)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.arc((18, 20, 70, 72), start=180, end=270, fill=DARK, width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (58, 24), "right", 10)


def z0(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Many cells: every unit its own zone.
    box(d, (4, 4, 91, 91), fill=accent)
    for k in (32, 60):
        line(d, [(k, 4), (k, 91)])
        line(d, [(4, k), (91, k)])


def z1(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Cells of one space type merged: two unit rows and a corridor.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 40), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 56, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 36, 92, 60))


def z2(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Perimeter ring split on the corner bisectors, plus a core.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92))
    d.rectangle((30, 30, 66, 66), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for a, b in [((4, 4), (30, 30)), ((92, 4), (66, 30)), ((4, 92), (30, 66)), ((92, 92), (66, 66))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def z3(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (6, 16, 90, 80), fill=accent)


def slab(d: Draw, accent, y: float, height: float = 16, left: float = 6, right: float = 90) -> None:
    box(d, (left, y, right, y + height), fill=accent, width=6)


def stack(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for y in (8, 36, 64):
        slab(d, accent, y, 24)


def multiplier(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    slab(d, accent, 6, 24, 6, 90)
    line(d, [(18, 44), (50, 84)], width=10)
    line(d, [(50, 44), (18, 84)], width=10)
    for y in (44, 58, 72):
        d.rectangle((62, y, 90, y + 8), fill=DARK)


def single_zone_per_floor_type(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One zone per floor entry: a one-storey zone below a three-storey zone.
    box(d, (24, 66, 71, 91), fill=accent)
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 58), fill=accent)


def single_zone_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 91), fill=accent)


def convert(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(40, 44), (90, 44), (90, 92), (40, 92)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(6, 14), (44, 14), (64, 34), (64, 56)], width=10)
    arrow_head(d, (64, 74), "down", 16)


def inspect(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 64, 64), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((18, 18, 38, 38), fill=WHITE)
    line(d, [(56, 56), (88, 88)], width=16)


def mini_grid(d: Draw, origin: tuple[float, float], size: float, cells: int, accent) -> None:
    x0, y0 = origin
    box(d, (x0, y0, x0 + size, y0 + size), fill=accent, width=6)
    step = size / cells
    for k in range(1, cells):
        line(d, [(x0 + k * step, y0), (x0 + k * step, y0 + size)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x0, y0 + k * step), (x0 + size, y0 + k * step)], width=4)


def map_source_target(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    mini_grid(d, (4, 4), 40, 2, accent)
    box(d, (52, 52, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(32, 56), (32, 72), (40, 72)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (52, 72), "right", 12)
    line(d, [(56, 26), (72, 26), (72, 36)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (72, 50), "down", 12)


def validate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(24, 50), (42, 68), (74, 30)], width=14, fill=WHITE)


# Parameter motifs: small versions of their object's motif, drawn inside the hexagon (x and y in 20..76).


def small_schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for x, h in ((28, 16), (40, 36), (52, 24), (64, 40)):
        d.rectangle((x, 72 - h, x + 7, 71), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 72, 75, 75), fill=DARK)


def small_load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(54, 20), (30, 52), (46, 52), (40, 76), (68, 44), (52, 44), (60, 20)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_preset(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(20, 36), (32, 24), (76, 24), (76, 72), (32, 72), (20, 60)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)
    d.ellipse((30, 42, 42, 54), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (20, 24, 75, 71), width=4)
    d.rectangle((24, 44, 71, 51), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 28, 31, 67), fill=DARK)
    for x in (48,):
        line(d, [(x, 24), (x, 44)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x, 52), (x, 71)], width=4)


def small_floor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (16, 36, 79, 59), fill=accent, width=4)


def small_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (32, 16, 63, 79), fill=accent, width=4)
    for y in (36, 56):
        line(d, [(32, y), (63, y)], width=4)


# --- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def component(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A component: its motif at full size."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        motif(ImageDraw.Draw(image), accent)

    return paint


def parameter(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A parameter: a small version of its object's motif inside a hexagon (the Grasshopper parameter shape)."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        d.polygon([(24, 4), (72, 4), (94, 48), (72, 92), (24, 92), (2, 48)], fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
        motif(d, accent)

    return paint


ICONS: dict[str, tuple[Colour, Painter]] = {
    "BemGenAssemblyInfo": (INFO, component(assembly)),
    "BemGenInfoComponent": (INFO, component(info)),
    "ScheduleComponent": (PROGRAM, component(schedule)),
    "LoadComponent": (PROGRAM, component(load)),
    "ProgramPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(preset_tag)),
    "DwellingUnitPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(house)),
    "CorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(corridor)),
    "StairPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(stair)),
    "OfficePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(office)),
    "CorePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(core)),
    "RetailPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(retail)),
    "MallPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(mall)),
    "KitchenPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(kitchen)),
    "DiningPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(dining)),
    "OperatingTheatrePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(operating_theatre)),
    "CleanCorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(clean_corridor)),
    "DirtyCorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(dirty_corridor)),
    "ClinicalSupportPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(clinical_support)),
    "CareBedroomPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(care_bedroom)),
    "CareCommunalPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(care_communal)),
    "LobbyPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(lobby)),
    "ActivityHallPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(activity_hall)),
    "ServicePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(service_rooms)),
    "LinearPlanGeneratorComponent": (GENERATE, component(linear_generator)),
    "StairBayBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_bay_bar)),
    "StairPairComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_pair)),
    "GalleryBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(gallery_bar)),
    "TerraceRowComponent": (GENERATE, component(terrace_row)),
    "PointPlateComponent": (GENERATE, component(point_plate)),
    "WingedBandComponent": (GENERATE, component(winged_band)),
    "OpenCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(open_court)),
    "EnclosedCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(enclosed_court)),
    "TransformPlanComponent": (GENERATE, component(transform_plan)),
    "NoSimplificationComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z0)),
    "SemanticMergeComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z1)),
    "PerimeterCoreComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z2)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z3)),
    "StackFloorsComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(stack)),
    "StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(multiplier)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_per_floor_type)),
    "SingleZoneBuildingComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_building)),
    "Convert2BemComponent": (CONVERT, component(convert)),
    "InspectComponent": (INSPECT, component(inspect)),
    "MapSourceToTargetComponent": (INSPECT, component(map_source_target)),
    "ValidateComponent": (INSPECT, component(validate)),
    "ScheduleParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_schedule)),
    "LoadParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_load)),
    "ProgramPresetParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_preset)),
    "PlanParameter": (GENERATE, parameter(small_plan)),
    "FloorParameter": (SIMPLIFY, parameter(small_floor)),
    "BuildingParameter": (AGGREGATE, parameter(small_building)),
}


def render(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    accent, paint = ICONS[name]
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (CANVAS, CANVAS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(image, accent)
    return image.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def classes_with_icons() -> set[str]:
    """Concrete classes that show an icon: components, parameters, and the assembly info."""
    pattern = re.compile(r"public sealed class (\w+) : (\w+)")
    names = set()
    for path in SOURCE.rglob("*.cs"):
        if "obj" in path.parts or "bin" in path.parts:
            continue
        for name, _ in pattern.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if name.endswith(("Component", "Parameter")) or name == "BemGenAssemblyInfo":
                names.add(name)
    return names


def main() -> int:
    expected = classes_with_icons()
    missing = sorted(expected - ICONS.keys())
    unknown = sorted(ICONS.keys() - expected)
    if missing or unknown:
        print(f"Classes without an icon: {missing}; icons without a class: {unknown}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stale = sorted(p.name for p in OUTPUT.glob("*.png") if p.stem not in ICONS)
    if stale:
        print(f"Icons without a class in {OUTPUT}: {stale}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    for name in sorted(ICONS):
        render(name).save(OUTPUT / f"{name}.png", optimize=False)
    print(f"Wrote {len(ICONS)} icons to {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [x] **Step 6: Look at the icons**

Draw a contact sheet of all icons at 8× (nearest neighbour) into the build folder, which git ignores:

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
icons = sorted(Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons").glob("*.png"))
cell = 24 * 8 + 16
sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (6 * cell, -(-len(icons) // 6) * cell), (235, 235, 235, 255))
for i, path in enumerate(icons):
    big = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA").resize((24 * 8, 24 * 8), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    sheet.alpha_composite(big, ((i % 6) * cell + 8, (i // 6) * cell + 8))
Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
sheet.save("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
print(len(icons), "icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
EOF
```

Expected: `51 icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png`.

Open the image. In the order of `sorted()` on Windows (case-insensitive), six per row: the fifteen new icons are orange like the residential presets. *Activity Hall Preset* (a sports court with its centre line and circle) is the first icon of the first row, which ends with *Care Bedroom Preset* (a bed) and *Care Communal Preset* (a round table with four people); the second row starts with *Clean Corridor Preset* (a corridor with a white route and an arrow) and *Clinical Support Preset* (shelves beside a small medical cross) and holds *Core Preset* (a box with two lift doors and an up and down arrow) fourth and *Dining Preset* (a plate between a fork and a knife) last; the third row starts with *Dirty Corridor Preset* (a corridor with a hatched route and an arrow); the fourth row starts with *Kitchen Preset* (a cooking pot) and ends with *Lobby Preset* (a glazed entrance front with an open door and an arrow) and *Mall Preset* (shop blocks along a white T of routes); the fifth row holds *Office Preset* (an office block with a grid of windows) third and *Operating Theatre Preset* (a medical cross) fifth; the sixth row holds *Retail Preset* (a shopping bag) fifth; the seventh row holds *Service Preset* (three lockers) third. The 36 icons of S8.3 keep their motifs and move to make room; the 51 icons fill eight rows of six and three icons of a ninth.

If the hashes of Step 5 differed (another Pillow version), this check decides: when every icon matches the description above, continue and record the Pillow version in the commit message body; otherwise stop and report the difference. The script is never edited to match hashes.

## Slice C — `feature/office-plate-cafeteria`

Each family is one task: the generator tests first (they fail to build), the generator, the snapshot reviewed and accepted, then the integration matrix, which passes at once because every simplifier and aggregator already handles the new plans; if a case of the matrix fails, stop and report: a pipeline change needs its own decision and test-first task. Task 9 adds the two components and their icons.

### Task 7: Office plate around a central service core (`SYN-TYP-017`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/OfficePlate/OfficePlateGenerator.cs`
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/OfficePlate/OfficePlateGeneratorTests.cs`, `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/OfficePlateTypologyTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/OfficePlate/Snapshots/office-plate-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (S2–S8.3): `PlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` (`protected PlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Validate`, `Layout`, `Describe`, `protected static bool IsPositive(double)`), `PlanParameters`, `PlanPresets.Slots`, `PresetSlot`, `PlanLayout(Polygon2 Footprint, IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone> Zones)`, `PlannedZone(ZoneId, string, SpaceType, Polygon2)`, `Polygon2.Rectangle`, `Polygon2.Create(outer, holes)`, `Provenance.Format`, `DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter`, `PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`; `SpaceType.Office` and `Core`, `ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Office` and `Core` (Task 5); test support `PlanAssertions` (`TilesTheFootprint`, `HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall`, `Neighbours`, `OutdoorFacings`), `Snapshot.Match`, `TextReport.Describe`, `Pipeline.Simplifier`, `Pipeline.Aggregator`, `Pipeline.Tolerances`, `PlanTransform.Apply`, `SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings, double)`, `NoSimplification`, `SingleZoneBuilding`, `FloorEntry`, `BuildingValidator`.
- Produces: `public sealed record OfficePlateParameters(double PlateLength, double PlateDepth, double CoreLength, double CoreDepth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (42, 30, 18, 6, 3.8, 0); `public sealed record OfficePlatePresets(ProgramPreset Office, ProgramPreset Core) : PlanPresets` (slots `Office`, `Core`); `public sealed class OfficePlateGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Name` = `OfficePlateGenerator`, `public const double MinimumOfficeDepth = 3.0`: with a plate `L × D` and a core `l × d`, the core `SC` (*Service Core*, `Core`) is `[(L − l)/2, (L + l)/2] × [(D − d)/2, (D + d)/2]` and the office `OF` (*Office*, `Office`) is the plate with the core as its one hole; the footprint is the plate, without a hole; errors `InvalidParameter` for each length that is not positive and finite (`{name} must be positive, got {value}.`) and, only when those pass, for each axis whose office depth `(L − l)/2` or `(D − d)/2` is below 3 m (`CoreLength 40 m leaves an office depth of 1 m at the west and east façades; SYN-TYP-017 needs at least 3 m of work area on every side of the core.`, and the same for `CoreDepth` at the south and north façades); provenance keys `PlateLength`, `PlateDepth`, `CoreLength`, `CoreDepth`. By default the core has 108 m² and no outdoor wall, the office 1152 m² and the four façades with one window each (218.88 m² of glazing).

- [x] **Step 1: Create the branch**

```bash
git switch -c feature/office-plate-cafeteria main
```

- [x] **Step 2: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/OfficePlate/OfficePlateGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

23 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical plan (zones `SC` and `OF`, names, space types, 1260 m², no footprint hole, 108 m² and 1152 m², the corners of the core, the office with the core as its one hole, 3.8 m storeys, no source zones); tiling for four plate and core sizes; the core without outdoor walls, adjoining only the office over 48 m; the office with every façade; the window rule (4 windows, 218.88 m², no diagnostic); five invalid lengths; a core too close to the west and east façades and to the south and north façades; an office depth of exactly 3 m; every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.OfficePlate;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.OfficePlate;

public sealed class OfficePlateGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly OfficePlatePresets Examples = new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Office, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Core);

    private readonly OfficePlateGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new OfficePlateParameters(PlateLength: 42.0, PlateDepth: 30.0, CoreLength: 18.0, CoreDepth: 6.0, FloorHeight: 3.8, OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            OfficePlateParameters.Default);
        Assert.Equal(3.0, OfficePlateGenerator.MinimumOfficeDepth);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlateIsOneOfficeRingAroundACentralCore()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "SC", "OF" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Service Core", "Office" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.Core, SpaceType.Office }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(42.0 * 30.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Empty(plan.Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 108.0, 1152.0 }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.FloorArea));
        Polygon2 core = plan.Zones[0].Parts[0].Footprint;
        Polygon2 office = plan.Zones[1].Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(12.0, 12.0), new Point2(30.0, 12.0), new Point2(30.0, 18.0), new Point2(12.0, 18.0) }, core.Outer);
        Assert.Empty(core.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(0.0, 0.0), new Point2(42.0, 0.0), new Point2(42.0, 30.0), new Point2(0.0, 30.0) }, office.Outer);
        IReadOnlyList<Point2> hole = Assert.Single(office.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(core.Outer.OrderBy(p => p.X).ThenBy(p => p.Y), hole.OrderBy(p => p.X).ThenBy(p => p.Y));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(3.8, z.Parts[0].Height));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(42.0, 30.0, 18.0, 6.0)]
    [InlineData(36.0, 36.0, 12.0, 12.0)]
    [InlineData(60.0, 20.5, 40.0, 8.5)]
    [InlineData(24.5, 18.0, 18.5, 12.0)]
    public void ZonesTileThePlate(double plateLength, double plateDepth, double coreLength, double coreDepth)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default with { PlateLength = plateLength, PlateDepth = plateDepth, CoreLength = coreLength, CoreDepth = coreDepth });

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal(plateLength * plateDepth, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(coreLength * coreDepth, plan.Zones[0].FloorArea, 9);
        Assert.Equal(2, plan.Zones.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheCoreHasNoOutdoorWallAndAdjoinsTheOfficeOnEverySide()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default);

        Assert.Empty(PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "SC"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "OF" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "SC"));
        WallSurface[] coreWalls = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "SC").ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(4, coreWalls.Length);
        Assert.All(coreWalls, w => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Interzone, w.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(2.0 * (18.0 + 6.0), coreWalls.Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
        Assert.Equal(4, plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Zone.Value == "OF" && w.AdjacentZone?.Value == "SC"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheOfficeHasEveryFacade()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "OF"));
        Assert.Equal(2.0 * (42.0 + 30.0), plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
        Assert.Equal(4, result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Windows.Count == 1));
        Assert.Equal(0.4 * 2.0 * (42.0 + 30.0) * 3.8, result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 30.0, 18.0, 6.0, "PlateLength")]
    [InlineData(42.0, -30.0, 18.0, 6.0, "PlateDepth")]
    [InlineData(42.0, 30.0, 0.0, 6.0, "CoreLength")]
    [InlineData(42.0, 30.0, 18.0, double.NaN, "CoreDepth")]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, 30.0, 18.0, 6.0, "PlateLength")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double plateLength, double plateDepth, double coreLength, double coreDepth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new OfficePlateParameters(plateLength, plateDepth, coreLength, coreDepth, 3.8, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ACoreTooCloseToTheWestAndEastFacadesIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default with { CoreLength = 40.0 }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(
            "CoreLength 40 m leaves an office depth of 1 m at the west and east façades; SYN-TYP-017 needs at least 3 m of work area on every side of the core.",
            error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ACoreTooCloseToTheSouthAndNorthFacadesIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default with { CoreDepth = 26.0 }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(
            "CoreDepth 26 m leaves an office depth of 2 m at the south and north façades; SYN-TYP-017 needs at least 3 m of work area on every side of the core.",
            error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnOfficeDepthOfExactlyTheMinimumIsValid()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default with { CoreLength = 36.0, CoreDepth = 24.0 }, Examples);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal((42.0 * 30.0) - (36.0 * 24.0), result.Value.Zones[1].FloorArea, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new OfficePlateParameters(0.0, -30.0, 0.0, 6.0, 0.0, double.NaN), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(5, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("Office", SpaceType.Office), ("Core", SpaceType.Core) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.Office, Examples.Core }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(
            OfficePlateParameters.Default,
            new OfficePlatePresets(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Core, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Office));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Office", "Core" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(OfficePlateGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "CoreDepth=6", "CoreLength=18", "FloorHeight=3.8", "OrientationDegrees=0", "PlateDepth=30", "PlateLength=42",
                "Presets=Office:Example Office;Core:Example Core",
            },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default)), "office-plate-canonical");
    }

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(OfficePlateParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/OfficePlate/OfficePlateGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.OfficePlate;

/// <summary>Parameters of the office plate around a central service core (SYN-TYP-017, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="PlateLength">Plate length along plan x, m.</param>
/// <param name="PlateDepth">Plate depth along plan y, m.</param>
/// <param name="CoreLength">Length of the central core along plan x, m.</param>
/// <param name="CoreDepth">Depth of the central core along plan y, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record OfficePlateParameters(
    double PlateLength,
    double PlateDepth,
    double CoreLength,
    double CoreDepth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: a 42 m × 30 m plate around an 18 m × 6 m core (12 m of office on every side), 3.8 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static OfficePlateParameters Default { get; } = new(42.0, 30.0, 18.0, 6.0, 3.8, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the office plate, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="Office">Preset of the office work area; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Office"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Core">Preset of the service core; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Core"/>.</param>
public sealed record OfficePlatePresets(ProgramPreset Office, ProgramPreset Core) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Office), SpaceType.Office, Office),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Core), SpaceType.Core, Core),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Central service core within an office plate (SYN-TYP-017, ADR-006): a rectangular plate whose office work area surrounds a central core on all
/// four sides (R001) and whose core holds the lifts and shared services (R002), a <see cref="SpaceType.Core"/> zone without an outdoor wall. The
/// work area is one department zone (D-097, ADR-014): the plate minus the core, a polygon with the core as its hole; the footprint has no hole.
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// Zone IDs: <c>SC</c> (the core), then <c>OF</c> (the office). With a plate <c>L × D</c> and a core <c>l × d</c>, the core is
/// <c>[(L − l)/2, (L + l)/2] × [(D − d)/2, (D + d)/2]</c>. The core must leave at least <see cref="MinimumOfficeDepth"/> of work area on every side
/// (R003/R004: a core near a façade is outside the family).
/// </remarks>
public sealed class OfficePlateGenerator : PlanGenerator<OfficePlateParameters, OfficePlatePresets>
{
    /// <summary>The smallest office depth between the core and a façade, m: a row of desks with its aisle (ADR-006).</summary>
    public const double MinimumOfficeDepth = 3.0;

    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public OfficePlateGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(OfficePlateGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(OfficePlateParameters parameters)
    {
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(OfficePlateParameters.PlateLength), parameters.PlateLength),
            (nameof(OfficePlateParameters.PlateDepth), parameters.PlateDepth),
            (nameof(OfficePlateParameters.CoreLength), parameters.CoreLength),
            (nameof(OfficePlateParameters.CoreDepth), parameters.CoreDepth),
        };
        var errors = values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToList();
        if (errors.Count == 0)
        {
            errors.AddRange(DepthError(nameof(OfficePlateParameters.CoreLength), parameters.CoreLength, parameters.PlateLength, "west and east"));
            errors.AddRange(DepthError(nameof(OfficePlateParameters.CoreDepth), parameters.CoreDepth, parameters.PlateDepth, "south and north"));
        }

        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(OfficePlateParameters p)
    {
        double west = (p.PlateLength - p.CoreLength) / 2.0;
        double south = (p.PlateDepth - p.CoreDepth) / 2.0;
        Polygon2 core = Polygon2.Rectangle(west, south, west + p.CoreLength, south + p.CoreDepth);
        Polygon2 plate = Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, p.PlateLength, p.PlateDepth);
        Polygon2 office = Polygon2.Create(plate.Outer, new[] { core.Outer }).Value;
        var zones = new[]
        {
            new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("SC"), "Service Core", SpaceType.Core, core),
            new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("OF"), "Office", SpaceType.Office, office),
        };
        return new PlanLayout(plate, zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(OfficePlateParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.PlateLength), Provenance.Format(p.PlateLength)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.PlateDepth), Provenance.Format(p.PlateDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CoreLength), Provenance.Format(p.CoreLength)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CoreDepth), Provenance.Format(p.CoreDepth)),
    };

    private static IEnumerable<Diagnostic> DepthError(string name, double core, double plate, string facades)
    {
        double depth = (plate - core) / 2.0;
        if (depth >= MinimumOfficeDepth)
        {
            yield break;
        }

        yield return Diagnostic.Error(
            DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
            $"{name} {Provenance.Format(core)} m leaves an office depth of {Provenance.Format(depth)} m at the {facades} façades; "
            + $"SYN-TYP-017 needs at least {Provenance.Format(MinimumOfficeDepth)} m of work area on every side of the core.");
    }
}
```
