- [x] **Step 8: Write the integration tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/OfficePlateTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

51 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (16), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (32, rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1) m, and rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m), `SemanticMergeKeepsTheOfficeRingAroundTheCore` (the office keeps its hole and 1152 m², the core no outdoor wall), `PerimeterCoreSplitsTheOfficeRingAndKeepsTheServiceCoreInItsCore` (`P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, `CORE`; `CORE` without a hole, of (42 − 9.14) × (30 − 9.14) m², sourced from `OF` and `SC`; every perimeter zone sourced from `OF` only), and `SingleZoneBuildingDropsTheRingAndKeepsEveryFacadeWindow` (one zone, no part with a hole, 20 outdoor walls with one window each). No production code changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.OfficePlate;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The S8.4 office plate (SYN-TYP-017, ADR-006): its work area is a ring around the core, the first zone with a hole in a footprint without one,
/// through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed.
/// </summary>
public sealed class OfficePlateTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeKeepsTheOfficeRingAroundTheCore()
    {
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.Core, SpaceType.Office }, floor.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType).OrderBy(t => t));
        Zone office = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Office);
        Assert.Single(office.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(1152.0, office.FloorArea, 6);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone == floor.Zones.Single(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Core).Id && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreSplitsTheOfficeRingAndKeepsTheServiceCoreInItsCore()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Assert.Equal("P-North,P-East,P-South,P-West,CORE", string.Join(",", floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Zone core = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE");
        Assert.Empty(core.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.Equal((42.0 - (2 * 4.57)) * (30.0 - (2 * 4.57)), core.FloorArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "OF", "SC" }, core.SourceZones.Select(z => z.Value).OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-", StringComparison.Ordinal)), z => Assert.Equal(new[] { "OF" }, z.SourceZones.Select(s => s.Value)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneBuildingDropsTheRingAndKeepsEveryFacadeWindow()
    {
        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZoneBuilding(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 3), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) }).Value;

        Zone zone = Assert.Single(building.Zones);
        Assert.All(zone.Parts, p => Assert.Empty(p.Footprint.Holes));
        Assert.Equal(5 * 4, building.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Windows.Count == 1));
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static OfficePlatePresets Presets() => new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Office, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Core);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan() => new OfficePlateGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(OfficePlateParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

### Task 8: Cafeteria service edge beside a dining field (`SYN-TYP-016`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/Cafeteria/CafeteriaGenerator.cs`
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/Cafeteria/CafeteriaGeneratorTests.cs`, `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/CafeteriaTypologyTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/Cafeteria/Snapshots/cafeteria-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: as Task 7, with `SpaceType.Kitchen` and `Dining` and `ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Kitchen` and `Dining` (Task 5).
- Produces: `public sealed record CafeteriaParameters(double Length, double DiningDepth, double KitchenDepth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (30, 15, 8, 4, 0); `public sealed record CafeteriaPresets(ProgramPreset Kitchen, ProgramPreset Dining) : PlanPresets` (slots `Kitchen`, `Dining`); `public sealed class CafeteriaGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Name` = `CafeteriaGenerator`: with length `L`, dining depth `Dd`, and kitchen depth `Dk`, `KS` (*Kitchen and Servery*, `Kitchen`) is `[0, L] × [Dd, Dd + Dk]` and `DI` (*Dining*, `Dining`) is `[0, L] × [0, Dd]`, so the dining field gets the south side; errors `InvalidParameter` for each length that is not positive and finite and, only when those pass, for a kitchen not shallower than the dining field (`KitchenDepth 15 m must be smaller than DiningDepth 15 m: in SYN-TYP-016 the dining field is the larger part of the plan.`); provenance keys `Length`, `DiningDepth`, `KitchenDepth`. By default 240 m² and 450 m², one 30 m interzone wall, and 6 windows (114.4 m² of glazing).

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/Cafeteria/CafeteriaGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

20 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical plan (zones `KS` and `DI`, their rectangles and areas); tiling for three sizes; the service edge sharing the dining field's whole north edge; the window rule (6 windows, no diagnostic); five invalid lengths; a kitchen as deep as or deeper than the dining field; every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.Cafeteria;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.Cafeteria;

public sealed class CafeteriaGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly CafeteriaPresets Examples = new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Kitchen, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Dining);

    private readonly CafeteriaGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new CafeteriaParameters(Length: 30.0, DiningDepth: 15.0, KitchenDepth: 8.0, FloorHeight: 4.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0), CafeteriaParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsAServiceEdgeAlongTheNorthOfTheDiningField()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "KS", "DI" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Kitchen and Servery", "Dining" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.Kitchen, SpaceType.Dining }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(30.0 * 23.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 240.0, 450.0 }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.FloorArea));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(0.0, 15.0), new Point2(30.0, 15.0), new Point2(30.0, 23.0), new Point2(0.0, 23.0) }, plan.Zones[0].Parts[0].Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(0.0, 0.0), new Point2(30.0, 0.0), new Point2(30.0, 15.0), new Point2(0.0, 15.0) }, plan.Zones[1].Parts[0].Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(4.0, z.Parts[0].Height));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(30.0, 15.0, 8.0)]
    [InlineData(12.5, 20.0, 4.5)]
    [InlineData(48.0, 9.0, 8.9)]
    public void ZonesTileThePlan(double length, double diningDepth, double kitchenDepth)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default with { Length = length, DiningDepth = diningDepth, KitchenDepth = kitchenDepth });

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal(length * (diningDepth + kitchenDepth), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(2, plan.Zones.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheServiceEdgeSharesTheDiningFieldsWholeNorthEdge()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default);

        WallSurface edge = Assert.Single(plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "KS" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone);
        Assert.Equal("DI", edge.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value);
        Assert.Equal(30.0, edge.Length, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "KS"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "DI"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
        Assert.Equal(6, result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Windows.Count == 1));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 15.0, 8.0, "Length")]
    [InlineData(30.0, -15.0, 8.0, "DiningDepth")]
    [InlineData(30.0, 15.0, 0.0, "KitchenDepth")]
    [InlineData(30.0, 15.0, double.NaN, "KitchenDepth")]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, 15.0, 8.0, "Length")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double length, double diningDepth, double kitchenDepth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new CafeteriaParameters(length, diningDepth, kitchenDepth, 4.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(15.0)]
    [InlineData(20.0)]
    public void AKitchenNotSmallerThanTheDiningFieldIsAnError(double kitchenDepth)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default with { KitchenDepth = kitchenDepth }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(
            $"KitchenDepth {kitchenDepth} m must be smaller than DiningDepth 15 m: in SYN-TYP-016 the dining field is the larger part of the plan.",
            error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new CafeteriaParameters(0.0, -15.0, 0.0, 0.0, double.NaN), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(5, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("Kitchen", SpaceType.Kitchen), ("Dining", SpaceType.Dining) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.Kitchen, Examples.Dining }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(
            CafeteriaParameters.Default,
            new CafeteriaPresets(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Dining, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Kitchen));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Kitchen", "Dining" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(CafeteriaGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "DiningDepth=15", "FloorHeight=4", "KitchenDepth=8", "Length=30", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=Kitchen:Example Kitchen;Dining:Example Dining" },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default)), "cafeteria-canonical");
    }

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(CafeteriaParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/Cafeteria/CafeteriaGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.Cafeteria;

/// <summary>Parameters of the cafeteria service edge beside a dining field (SYN-TYP-016, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="Length">Length of the service edge and the dining field along plan x, m.</param>
/// <param name="DiningDepth">Depth of the dining field along plan y, m.</param>
/// <param name="KitchenDepth">Depth of the kitchen and servery along plan y, m; smaller than <paramref name="DiningDepth"/>.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record CafeteriaParameters(
    double Length,
    double DiningDepth,
    double KitchenDepth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: a 30 m service edge, a 15 m deep dining field, an 8 m deep kitchen, 4 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static CafeteriaParameters Default { get; } = new(30.0, 15.0, 8.0, 4.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the cafeteria, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="Kitchen">Preset of the kitchen and servery; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Kitchen"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Dining">Preset of the dining field; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Dining"/>.</param>
public sealed record CafeteriaPresets(ProgramPreset Kitchen, ProgramPreset Dining) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Kitchen), SpaceType.Kitchen, Kitchen),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Dining), SpaceType.Dining, Dining),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Cafeteria service edge beside a dining field (SYN-TYP-016, ADR-006): food preparation and servery, one department zone (D-097, ADR-014), lie along
/// the whole north edge of the dining field (R001); the servery counter is the kitchen's front on that shared edge, so customers move past it within
/// the dining field into the dining space (R002), and the route is not a zone of its own. The dining field is the larger part (R003/R004).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// Zone IDs: <c>KS</c> (kitchen and servery, <c>[0, L] × [Dd, Dd + Dk]</c>), then <c>DI</c> (dining, <c>[0, L] × [0, Dd]</c>), for length
/// <c>L</c>, dining depth <c>Dd</c>, and kitchen depth <c>Dk</c>.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class CafeteriaGenerator : PlanGenerator<CafeteriaParameters, CafeteriaPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public CafeteriaGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(CafeteriaGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(CafeteriaParameters parameters)
    {
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(CafeteriaParameters.Length), parameters.Length),
            (nameof(CafeteriaParameters.DiningDepth), parameters.DiningDepth),
            (nameof(CafeteriaParameters.KitchenDepth), parameters.KitchenDepth),
        };
        var errors = values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToList();
        if (errors.Count == 0 && parameters.KitchenDepth >= parameters.DiningDepth)
        {
            errors.Add(Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(CafeteriaParameters.KitchenDepth)} {Provenance.Format(parameters.KitchenDepth)} m must be smaller than "
                + $"{nameof(CafeteriaParameters.DiningDepth)} {Provenance.Format(parameters.DiningDepth)} m: in SYN-TYP-016 the dining field is the larger part of the plan."));
        }

        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(CafeteriaParameters p)
    {
        double north = p.DiningDepth + p.KitchenDepth;
        var zones = new[]
        {
            new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("KS"), "Kitchen and Servery", SpaceType.Kitchen, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, p.DiningDepth, p.Length, north)),
            new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("DI"), "Dining", SpaceType.Dining, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, p.Length, p.DiningDepth)),
        };
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, p.Length, north), zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(CafeteriaParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Length), Provenance.Format(p.Length)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.DiningDepth), Provenance.Format(p.DiningDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.KitchenDepth), Provenance.Format(p.KitchenDepth)),
    };
}
```

- [x] **Step 7: Write the integration tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/CafeteriaTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

50 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (16), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (32), `SemanticMergeKeepsTheKitchenAndTheDiningFieldApart` (240 m² and 450 m²), and `PerimeterCoreMixesTheKitchenAndTheDiningFieldInTheNorthAndSidePerimeters` (`P-North` from `KS` only, `P-South` from `DI` only, `P-East` from both, every zone `Mixed`). No production code changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.Cafeteria;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>The S8.4 cafeteria (SYN-TYP-016, ADR-006) through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed.</summary>
public sealed class CafeteriaTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeKeepsTheKitchenAndTheDiningFieldApart()
    {
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.Kitchen, SpaceType.Dining }, floor.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType).OrderBy(t => t));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 240.0, 450.0 }, floor.Zones.OrderBy(z => z.SpaceType).Select(z => Math.Round(z.FloorArea, 6)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreMixesTheKitchenAndTheDiningFieldInTheNorthAndSidePerimeters()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Assert.Equal("P-North,P-East,P-South,P-West,CORE", string.Join(",", floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "KS" }, floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "P-North").SourceZones.Select(z => z.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DI" }, floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "P-South").SourceZones.Select(z => z.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DI", "KS" }, floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "P-East").SourceZones.Select(z => z.Value).OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(SpaceType.Mixed, z.SpaceType));
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static CafeteriaPresets Presets() => new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Kitchen, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Dining);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan() => new CafeteriaGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(CafeteriaParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: two components after *Enclosed Court* in the file, with their number inputs defaulting to the ADR-006 defaults, *Floor Height*, *Orientation*, one required preset input per space type (an unconnected one is Grasshopper's missing-input warning, D-065), and *Preview Location* last (D-062); `L`/`D` rather than `PL`, which is the nickname of *Preview Location*:

  | Component (nickname) | Inputs → output | Class | GUID |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | *Office Plate* (OPlate) | Plate Length (L, 42), Plate Depth (D, 30), Core Length (CL, 18), Core Depth (CD, 6), Floor Height (FH, 3.8), Orientation (O, 0), Office (Of), Core (Co), Preview Location (PL) → Plan | `OfficePlateComponent` | `783cefaa-b836-4dbc-b16d-8e74820ed361` |
  | *Cafeteria* (Cafe) | Length (L, 30), Dining Depth (DD, 15), Kitchen Depth (KD, 8), Floor Height (FH, 4), Orientation (O, 0), Kitchen (Ki), Dining (Di), Preview Location (PL) → Plan | `CafeteriaComponent` | `95956724-ddbc-48ce-9874-0ec6c244c8fe` |

  The motifs `office_plate` (a plate around a dark core) and `cafeteria` (a dark kitchen band above a dining field with tables), in the Generate green, mapped after `EnclosedCourtComponent`. 46 components, 6 parameters, and 53 icons. The eight earlier typology components and their base class are unchanged.

- [x] **Step 1: Replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (full file)**

Two usings and the two component classes at the end are added; nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.Cafeteria;
using Lod.Generators.EnclosedCourt;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Generators.OfficePlate;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Lod.Generators.WingedBand;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and output of the typology plan generator components (ADR-006): number inputs with the family's defaults, one required preset input
/// per space type (D-061, D-065), the Preview Location last (D-062), and one Plan output.
/// </summary>
public abstract class TypologyGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected const string FloorHeightDescription = "Floor-to-floor height, m.";

    protected const string OrientationDescription = "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.";

    protected const string Metres = " All lengths in metres regardless of model units.";

    protected TypologyGeneratorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description + Metres, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    /// <summary>Adds the required preset input of one space type.</summary>
    protected static void AddPreset(GH_InputParamManager pManager, string name, string nickname, SpaceType spaceType, string role) =>
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), name, nickname, $"Program preset of {role}; space type {spaceType}.", GH_ParamAccess.item);

    /// <summary>Reads <paramref name="count"/> number inputs from <paramref name="first"/>; <c>false</c> when one is missing.</summary>
    protected static bool TryNumbers(IGH_DataAccess DA, int first, int count, out double[] values)
    {
        values = new double[count];
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(first + i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>Reads a preset input; <c>null</c> when it is unconnected, which Grasshopper reports as a missing input (D-065).</summary>
    protected static ProgramPreset? Preset(IGH_DataAccess DA, int index)
    {
        ProgramPresetGoo? goo = null;
        return DA.GetData(index, ref goo) && goo is not null ? goo.Value : null;
    }

    /// <summary>Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the plan with this component's preview location.</summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IGeneratedPlan> result)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

public sealed class StairBayBarComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public StairBayBarComponent()
        : base(
            "Stair Bay Bar",
            "SBar",
            "Repeated stair bays in a linear bar (HDHM-TYP-001, ADR-006): bays of dwelling | stair | dwelling through the full depth, each stair serving the two dwellings beside it; no corridor. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("911c3204-42bb-4023-bdb7-2931ea1caad3");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        StairBayBarParameters d = StairBayBarParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Bay Count", "BC", "Number of stair bays along the bar; at least 2.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.BayCount);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Width of each dwelling along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.UnitWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Bar depth, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Width", "SW", "Width of each stair along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.StairWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the dwellings");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Stair", "St", SpaceType.Stair, "the stairs");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int bays = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref bays) || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 5, out double[] v) || Preset(DA, 6) is not { } dwellingUnit || Preset(DA, 7) is not { } stair)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new StairBayBarParameters(bays, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4]);
        Output(DA, new StairBayBarGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new StairBayBarPresets(dwellingUnit, stair)));
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 2: Build and see the icon script fail**

```bash
dotnet build BEMGen.sln -c Release
python scripts/make_icons.py
git status --short
```

Expected: `Build succeeded.`, `0 Warning(s)`, `0 Error(s)`; the script stops with exit code 1 and the message `Classes without an icon: ['CafeteriaComponent', 'OfficePlateComponent']; icons without a class: []`, and writes nothing (`git status --short` lists only ` M src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs`).

- [x] **Step 3: Replace `scripts/make_icons.py` (full file)**

The two motifs are added after `enclosed_court`, and their entries after *Enclosed Court* in the Generate group; every other motif and entry is unchanged.

```python
"""Draws the BEMGen Grasshopper icons (D-063).

Every icon is drawn at 96 x 96 pixels on a transparent background and downsampled to 24 x 24 with LANCZOS, then written to
src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/<Name>.png, where <Name> is the class name of the component, parameter, or assembly info that shows it.
Each icon is a dark glyph plus the accent colour of its toolbar panel; a parameter uses the panel of the component that makes its object.
Shapes only, no fonts, so the output does not depend on installed fonts.

Run from the repository root:  python scripts/make_icons.py
Requires Python 3.10+ and Pillow. Generated with Pillow 12.3.0; the same Pillow version gives the same pixels.
The script stops with an error when an icon has no class in src/Lod.Grasshopper or a class has no icon.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / "src" / "Lod.Grasshopper"
OUTPUT = SOURCE / "Icons"

CANVAS = 96
SIZE = 24
LINE = 8  # 2 px after downsampling

DARK = (38, 38, 38, 255)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)

# One accent per toolbar panel (ComponentCategories).
INFO = (96, 125, 139, 255)
PROGRAM = (230, 140, 30, 255)
GENERATE = (46, 158, 79, 255)
SIMPLIFY = (47, 123, 216, 255)
AGGREGATE = (138, 79, 200, 255)
CONVERT = (208, 69, 58, 255)
INSPECT = (24, 150, 150, 255)

Colour = tuple[int, int, int, int]
Draw = ImageDraw.ImageDraw
Motif = Callable[[Draw, Colour], None]
Painter = Callable[[Image.Image, Colour], None]


def box(d: Draw, xy: tuple[float, float, float, float], fill=None, outline=DARK, width: int = LINE) -> None:
    d.rectangle(xy, fill=fill, outline=outline, width=width)


def line(d: Draw, points: list[tuple[float, float]], width: int = LINE, fill=DARK) -> None:
    d.line(points, fill=fill, width=width, joint="curve")


def arrow_head(d: Draw, tip: tuple[float, float], direction: str, size: float, fill=DARK) -> None:
    x, y = tip
    if direction == "right":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x - size, y + size)], fill=fill)
    elif direction == "down":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x + size, y - size)], fill=fill)


# --- motifs: each draws inside the 96 x 96 canvas -------------------------------------------------------------


def info(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.rectangle((42, 40, 54, 74), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((42, 20, 54, 32), fill=WHITE)


def assembly(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A stepped block: the building at three levels of detail.
    box(d, (8, 52, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 30, 60, 56), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 8, 36, 34), fill=accent)


def schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A 24-hour profile as bars on a time axis.
    heights = [16, 48, 72, 36, 60, 76, 24]
    for i, h in enumerate(heights):
        x = 12 + i * 12
        d.rectangle((x, 84 - h, x + 7, 83), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((4, 84, 92, 91), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((4, 4, 11, 91), fill=DARK)


def load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    bolt = [(58, 4), (18, 54), (44, 54), (34, 92), (78, 38), (52, 38), (64, 4)]
    d.polygon(bolt, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)


def preset_tag(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    tag = [(8, 30), (30, 8), (88, 8), (88, 88), (30, 88), (8, 66)]
    d.polygon(tag, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((24, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(52, 34), (76, 34)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 48), (76, 48)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 62), (76, 62)], width=6)


def house(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(48, 6), (90, 44), (6, 44)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    box(d, (16, 42, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 60, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(16, 48), (60, 48)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (78, 48), "right", 12)


def stair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    steps = [(6, 90), (6, 70), (28, 70), (28, 50), (50, 50), (50, 30), (72, 30), (72, 10), (90, 10), (90, 90)]
    d.polygon(steps, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def office(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # An office block in elevation with a grid of windows.
    box(d, (16, 6, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (18, 38, 58):
        for x in (26, 52):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 16, y + 12), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((40, 76, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def core(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A service core: a box with two lift doors and an up and down arrow.
    box(d, (8, 8, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 34, 42, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((54, 34, 76, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.polygon([(48, 12), (38, 26), (58, 26)], fill=DARK)


def retail(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A shopping bag with its handle.
    d.arc((30, 6, 66, 46), start=180, end=360, fill=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.polygon([(14, 30), (82, 30), (90, 90), (6, 90)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A branching mall in plan: shop blocks (accent) along a T of public routes (white).
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 8, 56, 88), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((8, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE)
    for a, b in [((40, 4), (40, 40)), ((56, 4), (56, 92)), ((40, 56), (40, 92)), ((4, 40), (40, 40)), ((4, 56), (40, 56))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)


def kitchen(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A cooking pot with a lid and two handles.
    box(d, (16, 40, 80, 86), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(4, 50), (16, 50)])
    line(d, [(80, 50), (92, 50)])
    d.rectangle((12, 28, 84, 38), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((42, 16, 54, 28), fill=DARK)


def dining(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A plate between a fork and a knife.
    d.ellipse((26, 22, 74, 70), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((38, 34, 62, 58), outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(12, 14), (12, 86)], width=6)
    line(d, [(84, 14), (84, 86)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((80, 14, 88, 46), fill=DARK)


def operating_theatre(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A medical cross.
    d.polygon(
        [(34, 6), (62, 6), (62, 34), (90, 34), (90, 62), (62, 62), (62, 90), (34, 90), (34, 62), (6, 62), (6, 34), (34, 34)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=LINE,
    )


def clean_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a white (clean) route and an arrow.
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((10, 42, 70, 54), fill=WHITE)
    arrow_head(d, (86, 48), "right", 12)


def dirty_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a hatched (dirty) route and an arrow.
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in range(12, 66, 14):
        line(d, [(x, 60), (x + 10, 36)], width=5)
    arrow_head(d, (86, 48), "right", 12)


def clinical_support(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Shelves of supplies beside a small medical cross.
    box(d, (6, 10, 58, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (34, 58):
        line(d, [(6, y), (58, y)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((70, 28, 82, 64), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((58, 40, 94, 52), fill=DARK)


def care_bedroom(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bed in elevation: headboard, mattress, and pillow.
    d.rectangle((6, 24, 16, 86), fill=DARK)
    box(d, (16, 54, 90, 72), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 42, 42, 54), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(86, 72), (86, 86)])


def care_communal(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A round table with four people around it.
    d.ellipse((28, 28, 68, 68), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for x, y in [(48, 12), (84, 48), (48, 84), (12, 48)]:
        d.ellipse((x - 9, y - 9, x + 9, y + 9), fill=DARK)


def lobby(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A glazed entrance front with an open door and an arrow leading in.
    box(d, (6, 10, 90, 88), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(34, 10), (34, 88)], width=4)
    line(d, [(62, 10), (62, 88)], width=4)
    d.rectangle((38, 44, 58, 88), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    arrow_head(d, (48, 84), "down", 8)


def activity_hall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A sports court in plan: the hall, its centre line, and the centre circle.
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(48, 16), (48, 80)], width=5)
    d.ellipse((34, 34, 62, 62), outline=DARK, width=5)


def service_rooms(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A row of three lockers with their handles.
    for left in (6, 36, 66):
        box(d, (left, 8, left + 24, 88), fill=accent, width=6)
        d.rectangle((left + 15, 40, left + 19, 56), fill=DARK)


def linear_generator(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Footprint with a west stair, a corridor band, and dwelling-unit divisions on both sides.
    box(d, (4, 12, 91, 83))
    d.rectangle((12, 20, 23, 75), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((28, 40, 87, 55), fill=accent)
    for x in (48, 68):
        line(d, [(x, 12), (x, 40)])
        line(d, [(x, 55), (x, 83)])


def stair_bay_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A long bar of two stair bays: dwelling (accent) | stair (dark) | dwelling, repeated, with a party wall between the bays.
    box(d, (2, 30, 93, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in (20, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 30, x + 10, 66), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(48, 30), (48, 66)], width=4)


def stair_pair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One compact plate: two dwellings (accent) around one full-depth stair (dark).
    box(d, (14, 22, 82, 74), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((42, 26, 54, 70), fill=DARK)


def gallery_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bar of dwellings (accent) between two end stairs (dark); the gallery along it is dashed, as it is not modelled.
    box(d, (4, 34, 91, 76), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 38, 18, 72), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((77, 38, 87, 72), fill=DARK)
    for x in (36, 54):
        line(d, [(x, 34), (x, 76)], width=6)
    for x in range(6, 90, 16):
        line(d, [(x, 20), (x + 9, 20)], width=6)


def terrace_row(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Attached houses in elevation: three gables sharing their side walls.
    houses = [(6, 34), (34, 62), (62, 90)]
    for left, right in houses:
        middle = (left + right) / 2
        d.polygon([(left, 46), (middle, 22), (right, 46), (right, 88), (left, 88)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for left, right in houses:
        d.rectangle((left + 10, 68, right - 10, 88), fill=DARK)


def point_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A square plate: four dwellings (accent) in a pinwheel around one central core (dark) without façades.
    box(d, (6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((36, 36, 60, 60), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((6, 36), (60, 36)), ((60, 6), (60, 60)), ((36, 60), (90, 60)), ((36, 36), (36, 90))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def winged_band(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A comb in plan: a band (bottom) with three wings projecting north (up), every wing joined to the band along its root; stairs dark.
    d.polygon(
        [(4, 90), (92, 90), (92, 8), (70, 8), (70, 62), (59, 62), (59, 8), (37, 8), (37, 62), (26, 62), (26, 8), (4, 8)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=6,
    )
    for left, right in ((4, 26), (37, 59), (70, 92)):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 30, right - 3, 38), fill=DARK)
    for x in (20, 48, 76):
        d.rectangle((x, 66, x + 6, 87), fill=DARK)


def open_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A U in plan: a north wing (top) across the full width and two side wings, around a court open to the south (bottom); stairs dark.
    d.polygon([(4, 92), (28, 92), (28, 34), (68, 34), (68, 92), (92, 92), (92, 6), (4, 6)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for x in (24, 45, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 9, x + 6, 31), fill=DARK)
    for left in (4, 68):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 58, left + 21, 66), fill=DARK)


def enclosed_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A closed ring in plan: south and north wings across the full width and west and east wings between them, around a court (the hole,
    # transparent) closed on all four sides; stairs dark.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (30, 30, 66, 66), fill=(0, 0, 0, 0), width=6)
    for y in (7, 69):
        for x in (24, 66):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 6, y + 20), fill=DARK)
    for left in (7, 69):
        d.rectangle((left, 45, left + 20, 51), fill=DARK)


def office_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A rectangular plate: one office work area (accent) all around a central service core (dark).
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((32, 40, 64, 56), fill=DARK)


def cafeteria(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A rectangle: the kitchen and servery along the north edge (dark) and the dining field (accent) with tables.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 14, 88, 34), fill=DARK)
    for y in (48, 68):
        for x in (20, 44, 68):
            d.ellipse((x, y, x + 10, y + 10), fill=DARK)


def transform_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A footprint outline and the same footprint rotated and moved, joined by a turning arrow.
    box(d, (6, 52, 50, 90), width=6)
    d.polygon([(66, 12), (92, 28), (76, 56), (50, 40)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.arc((18, 20, 70, 72), start=180, end=270, fill=DARK, width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (58, 24), "right", 10)


def z0(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Many cells: every unit its own zone.
    box(d, (4, 4, 91, 91), fill=accent)
    for k in (32, 60):
        line(d, [(k, 4), (k, 91)])
        line(d, [(4, k), (91, k)])


def z1(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Cells of one space type merged: two unit rows and a corridor.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 40), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 56, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 36, 92, 60))


def z2(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Perimeter ring split on the corner bisectors, plus a core.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92))
    d.rectangle((30, 30, 66, 66), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for a, b in [((4, 4), (30, 30)), ((92, 4), (66, 30)), ((4, 92), (30, 66)), ((92, 92), (66, 66))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def z3(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (6, 16, 90, 80), fill=accent)


def slab(d: Draw, accent, y: float, height: float = 16, left: float = 6, right: float = 90) -> None:
    box(d, (left, y, right, y + height), fill=accent, width=6)


def stack(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for y in (8, 36, 64):
        slab(d, accent, y, 24)


def multiplier(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    slab(d, accent, 6, 24, 6, 90)
    line(d, [(18, 44), (50, 84)], width=10)
    line(d, [(50, 44), (18, 84)], width=10)
    for y in (44, 58, 72):
        d.rectangle((62, y, 90, y + 8), fill=DARK)


def single_zone_per_floor_type(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One zone per floor entry: a one-storey zone below a three-storey zone.
    box(d, (24, 66, 71, 91), fill=accent)
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 58), fill=accent)


def single_zone_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 91), fill=accent)


def convert(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(40, 44), (90, 44), (90, 92), (40, 92)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(6, 14), (44, 14), (64, 34), (64, 56)], width=10)
    arrow_head(d, (64, 74), "down", 16)


def inspect(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 64, 64), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((18, 18, 38, 38), fill=WHITE)
    line(d, [(56, 56), (88, 88)], width=16)


def mini_grid(d: Draw, origin: tuple[float, float], size: float, cells: int, accent) -> None:
    x0, y0 = origin
    box(d, (x0, y0, x0 + size, y0 + size), fill=accent, width=6)
    step = size / cells
    for k in range(1, cells):
        line(d, [(x0 + k * step, y0), (x0 + k * step, y0 + size)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x0, y0 + k * step), (x0 + size, y0 + k * step)], width=4)


def map_source_target(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    mini_grid(d, (4, 4), 40, 2, accent)
    box(d, (52, 52, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(32, 56), (32, 72), (40, 72)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (52, 72), "right", 12)
    line(d, [(56, 26), (72, 26), (72, 36)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (72, 50), "down", 12)


def validate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(24, 50), (42, 68), (74, 30)], width=14, fill=WHITE)


# Parameter motifs: small versions of their object's motif, drawn inside the hexagon (x and y in 20..76).


def small_schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for x, h in ((28, 16), (40, 36), (52, 24), (64, 40)):
        d.rectangle((x, 72 - h, x + 7, 71), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 72, 75, 75), fill=DARK)


def small_load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(54, 20), (30, 52), (46, 52), (40, 76), (68, 44), (52, 44), (60, 20)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_preset(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(20, 36), (32, 24), (76, 24), (76, 72), (32, 72), (20, 60)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)
    d.ellipse((30, 42, 42, 54), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (20, 24, 75, 71), width=4)
    d.rectangle((24, 44, 71, 51), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 28, 31, 67), fill=DARK)
    for x in (48,):
        line(d, [(x, 24), (x, 44)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x, 52), (x, 71)], width=4)


def small_floor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (16, 36, 79, 59), fill=accent, width=4)


def small_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (32, 16, 63, 79), fill=accent, width=4)
    for y in (36, 56):
        line(d, [(32, y), (63, y)], width=4)


# --- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def component(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A component: its motif at full size."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        motif(ImageDraw.Draw(image), accent)

    return paint


def parameter(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A parameter: a small version of its object's motif inside a hexagon (the Grasshopper parameter shape)."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        d.polygon([(24, 4), (72, 4), (94, 48), (72, 92), (24, 92), (2, 48)], fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
        motif(d, accent)

    return paint

    "SingleZoneBuildingComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_building)),
    "Convert2BemComponent": (CONVERT, component(convert)),
    "InspectComponent": (INSPECT, component(inspect)),

    "ScheduleParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_schedule)),
    "LoadParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_load)),
    "ProgramPresetParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_preset)),
    "PlanParameter": (GENERATE, parameter(small_plan)),
    "FloorParameter": (SIMPLIFY, parameter(small_floor)),
    "BuildingParameter": (AGGREGATE, parameter(small_building)),
}


def render(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    accent, paint = ICONS[name]
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (CANVAS, CANVAS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(image, accent)
    return image.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def classes_with_icons() -> set[str]:
    """Concrete classes that show an icon: components, parameters, and the assembly info."""
    pattern = re.compile(r"public sealed class (\w+) : (\w+)")
    names = set()
    for path in SOURCE.rglob("*.cs"):
        if "obj" in path.parts or "bin" in path.parts:
            continue
        for name, _ in pattern.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if name.endswith(("Component", "Parameter")) or name == "BemGenAssemblyInfo":
                names.add(name)
    return names


def main() -> int:
    expected = classes_with_icons()
    missing = sorted(expected - ICONS.keys())
    unknown = sorted(ICONS.keys() - expected)
    if missing or unknown:
        print(f"Classes without an icon: {missing}; icons without a class: {unknown}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stale = sorted(p.name for p in OUTPUT.glob("*.png") if p.stem not in ICONS)
    if stale:
        print(f"Icons without a class in {OUTPUT}: {stale}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    for name in sorted(ICONS):
        render(name).save(OUTPUT / f"{name}.png", optimize=False)
    print(f"Wrote {len(ICONS)} icons to {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

```bash
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
icons = sorted(Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons").glob("*.png"))
cell = 24 * 8 + 16
sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (6 * cell, -(-len(icons) // 6) * cell), (235, 235, 235, 255))
for i, path in enumerate(icons):
    big = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA").resize((24 * 8, 24 * 8), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    sheet.alpha_composite(big, ((i % 6) * cell + 8, (i // 6) * cell + 8))
Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
sheet.save("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
print(len(icons), "icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
EOF
```

1. Replace

```markdown
At Checkpoint 1 (stages S0–S4, D-030) the pipeline is complete for the linear plan; the revision stage S4.1 (D-061 to D-064) gave the plan generator one preset input per space type and added default preset components, a *Preview Location* input, and component icons; the revision stage S4.2 (D-068 to D-075, [ADR-013](../decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md)) added *Transform Plan*, made the floor aggregators designate floors and ceilings on the assembled stack, turned them into four methods, and made heat transfer between zones a conversion option; stage S5 (D-079, [ADR-012](../decisions/ADR-012-centred-windows.md)) gives every rebuilt outdoor wall one centred window with the glazed area it covers; stage S8.1 (D-082 to D-084, [ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md), [ADR-011](../decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md)) adds a plan generator for each bar family of the *High-Density Housing* precedent study and the minimum wall and window geometry of every generator; stage S8.2 (D-084) adds the non-rectangular families without holes, the point plate, the winged band, and the open court, and makes the united zones and the edge tests of the surface builder exact on rotated plans; stage S8.3 (D-084) adds the enclosed court, the first footprint with a hole, which every simplifier and aggregator supports. This document describes each step, the rules it applies, where results are validated, and the Grasshopper components that expose it. The domain types are described in [domain-model.md](domain-model.md), the validation checks in [validation.md](validation.md), and the `Convert2BEM` output layout in [convert2bem.md](convert2bem.md).
```

The default preset components *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset* take these values as their input defaults, so each works with nothing connected, and each shows the remark "Illustrative values; not sourced from DOE prototypes or standards."

   with

```markdown
- `ExampleResidentialPresets` hold illustrative round numbers for development and tests, not DOE prototype values: dwelling unit and corridor conditioned, stair unconditioned. Each is available as values (`DwellingUnitValues`, `CorridorValues`, `StairValues`) and as the preset built from them (`DwellingUnit`, `Corridor`, `Stair`). The default preset components *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset* take these values as their input defaults, so each works with nothing connected, and each shows the remark "Illustrative values; not sourced from DOE prototypes or standards."
- `ExampleNonResidentialPresets` (S8.4, ADR-014) hold illustrative presets, all conditioned, for the space types of the non-residential departments (D-097): `Office`, `Core`, `Retail`, `Mall`, `Kitchen`, `Dining`, `OperatingTheatre`, `CleanCorridor`, `DirtyCorridor`, `ClinicalSupport`, `CareBedroom`, `CareCommunal`, `Lobby`, `ActivityHall`, and `Service`; the kitchen has gas equipment and, like the operating theatre, ventilation in air changes per hour; the retail schedules have their own Saturday and Sunday profiles. One default preset component per type, *Office Preset* to *Service Preset*, takes these values as input defaults with the same remark. Values and schedules: [program presets](../research/program-presets.md) §2.2.
```

1. Replace

```markdown
**Footprints with holes (S8.3).** A court is a hole of the footprint, outside the building: its edges are outdoor walls with windows by the generator's rule, it has no zone, and no floor or ceiling covers it, so it adds neither ground nor roof area. Every plan simplifier and floor aggregator supports such footprints ([ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md) for *Perimeter Core*, [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md) for the floor aggregators). In Grasshopper a polygon with holes is one planar face with an inner loop per hole, and a zone part one closed extrusion of that face.
```

   with

```markdown
**Footprints with holes (S8.3).** A court is a hole of the footprint, outside the building: its edges are outdoor walls with windows by the generator's rule, it has no zone, and no floor or ceiling covers it, so it adds neither ground nor roof area. Every plan simplifier and floor aggregator supports such footprints ([ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md) for *Perimeter Core*, [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md) for the floor aggregators). In Grasshopper a polygon with holes is one planar face with an inner loop per hole, and a zone part one closed extrusion of that face. A zone may also have a hole inside a footprint without one: the office work area of `SYN-TYP-017` (S8.4, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)) is a ring around the core; the hole's edges are interzone walls with the zone inside it, and no step needed a change for it.
```

```markdown
- Not modelled: individual work areas, meeting rooms, and the rooms of the core (D-097); a core off the centre; non-rectangular plates. *Perimeter Core* still needs every part of the plate wider than twice its depth (D-087); the default plate passes with 4.57 m.
```

   with

```markdown
- Not modelled: individual work areas, meeting rooms, and the rooms of the core (D-097); a core off the centre; non-rectangular plates. *Perimeter Core* still needs every part of the plate wider than twice its depth (D-087); the default plate passes with 4.57 m.
- Implemented (S8.4): as specified. Zones `SC` (*Service Core*), `OF` (*Office*); by default 108 m² and 1152 m², 4 windows of WWR 0.4 (218.88 m² of glazing), none on the core, whose four walls are all interzone walls with the office. The office's hole is the core's ring, so the office has eight walls: four outdoor and four facing the core. *Semantic Merge* keeps the two zones (the ring keeps its hole); *Perimeter Core* gives `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, and a `CORE` of 32.86 m × 20.86 m (685.4596 m²) that contains the whole service core and the inner part of the ring; the single-zone methods drop the ring.
```

   with

```markdown
- Not modelled: the counter, the queue, the kitchen's rooms (stores, wash-up), seating, and entrances (D-097); a service edge on more than one side.
- Implemented (S8.4): as specified. Zones `KS` (*Kitchen and Servery*, 240 m²) and `DI` (*Dining*, 450 m²), sharing one 30 m interzone wall; 6 windows (114.4 m² of glazing), three per zone. *Perimeter Core* puts the kitchen alone in `P-North`, the dining field alone in `P-South`, and both in `P-East`, `P-West`, and `CORE`.

#### Pipeline consequences of S8.4

The office plate's work area is the first generated zone with a hole in a footprint without one. The surface builder pairs the hole's edges with the core's edges as interzone walls, and every plan simplifier and floor aggregator handles the ring as it handles the united rings of *Semantic Merge* (S8.2): every combination validates for both families, canonical and at the two rotated placements of the test suite (and, in a sweep run while building the reference, at 50 rotations from 0° to 357.7° for the default plans and for an office plate with the 3 m minimum depth). No step of `Lod.Core` changed, and no tolerance or check was relaxed.
```

   with

```markdown
A program preset carries everything BEMGen assigns to a space of one type: its loads with their schedules, whether it is conditioned and, if so, its heating and cooling setpoints, and its window parameter (D-024, D-038). There is one preset per space type. Rooms are never modelled: at the most detailed level (Z0) a dwelling unit is one zone per storey (D-009, D-085), and a non-residential floor has one zone per department, a contiguous group of rooms of one program (D-097, ADR-014).
```


   with

```markdown
### 2.2 Non-residential presets (S8.4)

> **The values below are illustrative, like those of §2.1: plausible round numbers for development and tests, NOT taken from the DOE prototype buildings, from any standard, or from measured data.** Their reasons are recorded in [ADR-014](../decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md).

[`ExampleNonResidentialPresets`](../../src/Lod.Core/Programs/ExampleNonResidentialPresets.cs) defines one preset for each space type that the non-residential families place (D-094, D-097): the twelve new types and `Core`, `Lobby`, and `Service`. Each is available as `PresetValues` (`OfficeValues`, …, `ServiceValues`) and as the preset built from them (`Office`, …, `Service`); `SpaceTypes`, `ValuesOf(SpaceType)`, and `PresetOf(SpaceType)` list and look them up. Every one is conditioned. The Grasshopper components *Office Preset* to *Service Preset* use these values as the defaults of their inputs (§5).
```

```markdown
S4.1 adds one default preset component per space type of the linear plan, *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset*, and deletes *Example Residential Presets* (D-061). Every input of a default preset component has a default, the values of §2, so the component works with nothing connected: Name, WWR, Conditioned, Heating (°C), Cooling (°C), and per load a value input (people/m², W/m², or 1/h, as in its description) and an optional schedule input that replaces the built-in schedule when connected. Each shows the remark "Illustrative values; not sourced from DOE prototypes or standards." Their inputs and outputs are listed in [pipeline.md](../architecture/pipeline.md#grasshopper-components-through-s83).
```

```markdown
S4.1 adds one default preset component per space type of the linear plan, *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset*, and deletes *Example Residential Presets* (D-061). Every input of a default preset component has a default, the values of §2, so the component works with nothing connected: Name, WWR, Conditioned, Heating (°C), Cooling (°C), and per load a value input (people/m², W/m², or 1/h, as in its description) and an optional schedule input that replaces the built-in schedule when connected. Each shows the remark "Illustrative values; not sourced from DOE prototypes or standards." S8.4 adds one default preset component per non-residential space type of §2.2, *Office Preset*, *Core Preset*, *Retail Preset*, *Mall Preset*, *Kitchen Preset*, *Dining Preset*, *Operating Theatre Preset*, *Clean Corridor Preset*, *Dirty Corridor Preset*, *Clinical Support Preset*, *Care Bedroom Preset*, *Care Communal Preset*, *Lobby Preset*, *Activity Hall Preset*, and *Service Preset*, with the same inputs; *Gas Equipment* is in W/m², and *Ventilation* and *Infiltration* are in 1/h. Their inputs and outputs are listed in [pipeline.md](../architecture/pipeline.md#grasshopper-components-through-s84).
```

Append this section after the last line of the file (after the S8.3 checklist), separated by one blank line, so that it nests under `## Stage checklists` like the S2–S8.3 checklists:

```markdown
### S8.4 checklist — non-residential programs, office plate, and cafeteria (`v0.8.3`)

S8.4 adds fifteen default preset components in *1 Program* for the non-residential space types ([ADR-014](../decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)) and two plan generator components in *2 Generate* for the office plate (`SYN-TYP-017`) and the cafeteria (`SYN-TYP-016`) ([ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)). No existing component, input, output, or GUID changes.

#### Toolbar and icons

- [ ] *1 Program* shows, after *Stair Preset*, *Office Preset*, *Core Preset*, *Retail Preset*, *Mall Preset*, *Kitchen Preset*, *Dining Preset*, *Operating Theatre Preset*, *Clean Corridor Preset*, *Dirty Corridor Preset*, *Clinical Support Preset*, *Care Bedroom Preset*, *Care Communal Preset*, *Lobby Preset*, *Activity Hall Preset*, and *Service Preset*, each with its own orange icon.
- [ ] *2 Generate* shows *Office Plate* (a plate around a dark core) and *Cafeteria* (a dark kitchen band above a dining field with tables), each with its own green icon.
- [ ] Each preset component works with nothing connected, outputs one *Preset*, and shows the remark "Illustrative values; not sourced from DOE prototypes or standards."
- [ ] *Kitchen Preset* inputs, in order: Name (Example Kitchen), WWR (0.1), Conditioned (True), Heating (18), Cooling (26), Occupancy (0.05), Occupancy Schedule, Lighting (12), Lighting Schedule, Equipment (30), Equipment Schedule, Gas Equipment (40), Gas Equipment Schedule, Ventilation (15), Ventilation Schedule, Infiltration (0.3), Infiltration Schedule; the descriptions give W/m² for *Gas Equipment* and 1/h for *Ventilation*.
- [ ] *Operating Theatre Preset* defaults: WWR 0, Heating 20, Cooling 22, Ventilation 20; *Care Bedroom Preset*: Heating 22, Cooling 25.
- [ ] *Office Plate* (OPlate) inputs, in order, with their defaults: Plate Length 42, Plate Depth 30, Core Length 18, Core Depth 6, Floor Height 3.8, Orientation 0, Office, Core, Preview Location; one output, *Plan*.
- [ ] *Cafeteria* (Cafe) inputs: Length 30, Dining Depth 15, Kitchen Depth 8, Floor Height 4, Orientation 0, Kitchen, Dining, Preview Location; one output, *Plan*.
- [ ] With a preset input unconnected, each generator shows Grasshopper's missing-input warning and outputs nothing (D-065); *Office Preset* on the *Core* input gives `Error PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`.

#### Default plans

1. Place *Office Plate* with *Office Preset* and *Core Preset* connected; connect *Inspect* to its *Plan*.
   - [ ] No runtime message; zones `SC` (*Service Core*, 108 m²) and `OF` (*Office*, 1152 m²) in a 42 m × 30 m plate; the preview shows the office as a ring around the core at x = 12–30 m, y = 12–18 m.
   - [ ] 4 windows, 218.88 m² of glazing (63.84 north, 63.84 south, 45.6 east, 45.6 west in *Validate* on its *No Simplification* floor); the core has no outdoor wall and no window.
   - [ ] The *Inspect* report equals the snapshot `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/OfficePlate/Snapshots/office-plate-canonical.txt`.
2. Place *Cafeteria* with *Kitchen Preset* and *Dining Preset* connected; connect *Inspect*.
```

```markdown
Checkpoint 1 (D-030) is reached: stages S0–S4 of the [implementation roadmap](docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) are complete (tag `v0.4.0`), and so are the revision stages S4.1 (tag `v0.4.1`, D-064) and S4.2 (tag `v0.4.2`, D-073), stage S5 (tag `v0.5.0`, D-079), and sub-stages S8.1 (tag `v0.8.0`), S8.2 (tag `v0.8.1`), S8.3 (tag `v0.8.2`, D-084), and S8.4 (tag `v0.8.3`, D-101). The pipeline runs end to end for eleven plan generators: program presets (the residential `ExampleResidentialPresets` and, for the non-residential departments of [ADR-014](docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md), `ExampleNonResidentialPresets`, one typed preset and default preset component per space type) → a plan generator (one `PlanGenerator` subclass per typology family with typed parameter and preset records, [ADR-006](docs/decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md); `SYN-TYP-001` to `009` are the *High-Density Housing* families `HDHM-TYP-001` to `009`, D-099: `LinearPlanGenerator`, the double-sided internal corridor bar `SYN-TYP-009`; the bar families `StairBayBarGenerator` 001, `StairPairGenerator` 008, `GalleryBarGenerator` 002 with its gallery not modelled, and `TerraceRowGenerator` 006 with one dwelling zone per plot and storey, D-085; the non-rectangular families `PointPlateGenerator` 007, a square plate whose core has no outdoor wall, `WingedBandGenerator` 003, a comb, and `OpenCourtGenerator` 004, a U; `EnclosedCourtGenerator` 005, four wings around a court closed on all four sides, the first footprint with a hole; and the non-residential `OfficePlateGenerator` `SYN-TYP-017`, whose office work area is one ring-shaped zone around a central `Core` zone, and `CafeteriaGenerator` `SYN-TYP-016`, a `Kitchen` zone along the whole north edge of a larger `Dining` zone, each zoned one zone per department, D-097; one typed preset per space type, D-061; optionally rotated and moved by `PlanTransform`, D-070) → plan simplifiers (`NoSimplification`, `SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore`, `SingleZonePerFloor`) → floor aggregators (`Stack`, `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, `SingleZoneBuilding`, D-071; floors and ceilings designated on the assembled stack, D-068) → validation → `Convert2BEM` (heat transfer between zones an export option, off by default, D-069), as described in [docs/architecture/pipeline.md](docs/architecture/pipeline.md). Every generator places one centred window per outdoor wall (D-026), except on a wall shorter than 1 m or where the window would be narrower or lower than 0.3 m (`GeometryLimits`, info `WindowOmitted`, [ADR-011](docs/decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md)). Wherever zones are merged and walls are rebuilt (`SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore`, `SingleZonePerFloor`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, `SingleZoneBuilding`), every outdoor wall carries one centred window with the glazed area of the source windows it covers (`CentredWindows`, D-079, [ADR-012](docs/decisions/ADR-012-centred-windows.md)); the other steps keep their input's windows. Merged zones are unions of their sources as tiles that keep the sources' own vertices, so they may have holes and stay exact on rotated plans, and vertices on edges are recognised at their original positions (D-088, [ADR-002](docs/decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md)). Every step supports footprints with holes: a court is outside the building, its edges are outdoor walls facing into it, every footprint edge, court edges included, must be covered by outdoor zone edges (ADR-002), and `PerimeterCore` gives the court façades their own zones `P-Court-<Bin>` around a core that is a ring ([ADR-008](docs/decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)); `PerimeterCore` needs every part of a footprint, including the wings between the outer and the court façades, to be wider than twice its depth and otherwise fails with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087). Vertical aggregation follows [ADR-013](docs/decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md), which supersedes [ADR-009](docs/decisions/ADR-009-vertical-aggregation.md) where it differs. In *Convert2BEM* and the previews, zone solids are outward-oriented and every surface and window faces out of its zone (D-091). ADR-006, ADR-011, ADR-012, ADR-013, ADR-014, and the S4.2, S5, S8.1, S8.2, S8.3, and S8.4 plans were accepted by the controller and await the owner's reading (D-075, D-080, D-084, D-098). In Grasshopper every component has an icon drawn by `scripts/make_icons.py` (D-063), and the generator, *Transform Plan*, simplifier, and aggregator components have a *Preview Location* input that moves only their preview (D-062). Until the owner says otherwise, changes need not keep backward compatibility, but component GUIDs never change (D-064).

The precedent set is the four-source family set `SYN-TYP-001` to `SYN-TYP-019` of every program type, housing and non-residential ([docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md](docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md), D-093, D-094); `SYN-TYP-013` is set aside (D-096), non-residential floors are zoned one zone per department (D-097, [ADR-014](docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)), and BEMGen documents use the `SYN-TYP-*` IDs (D-099). The families `017` and `016` were built in S8.4; `010`, `014`, `018`, `019` (S8.5), `015`, `011` (S8.6), and `012` (S8.7) are specified in ADR-006 and built before S6 (D-098), the hub-and-arms families with at most four arms because the shared layout is axis-aligned (D-100).

Not implemented yet: the four-source families of S8.5–S8.7, `Lod.Export` and Convert2IDF (S6), and Grasshopper UX and release (S9). The precedent study of S7 was carried out in a separate repository (D-082); its nine families are paraphrased in [docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md](docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md). A zone only about 1 m wide on a rotated plan can fail validation after a merging simplifier, a known limit of clipping on the grid (D-090). The example program presets hold illustrative values, not DOE prototype values, and the `Convert2BEM` output layout stays provisional until a ClimateStudio reference definition exists (D-023).
```

In `README.md`, replace everything between the heading `## Status` and the heading `## What it does` (both headings stay) with:

```markdown
Checkpoint 1 reached: stages S0–S4 of the [implementation roadmap](docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) are complete (tag `v0.4.0`), followed by the revision stages S4.1 (tag `v0.4.1`) and S4.2 (tag `v0.4.2`), stage S5 (tag `v0.5.0`), and sub-stages S8.1 (tag `v0.8.0`), S8.2 (tag `v0.8.1`), S8.3 (tag `v0.8.2`), and S8.4 (tag `v0.8.3`). In Rhino 8 the plugin runs the whole pipeline for eleven plan generators: program presets with conditioning (a default preset component per space type, housing and non-residential); the linear plan generator and the eight other typologies of the *High-Density Housing* precedent study (`SYN-TYP-001`–`009`, ADR-006), the bars *Stair Bay Bar*, *Stair Pair*, *Gallery Bar*, and *Terrace Row*, the non-rectangular *Point Plate*, *Winged Band*, and *Open Court*, *Enclosed Court*, whose footprint has the court as a hole, and the non-residential *Office Plate* (`SYN-TYP-017`, a work-area ring around a central core) and *Cafeteria* (`SYN-TYP-016`, a kitchen and servery edge beside a dining field), each zoned one zone per department (D-097, ADR-014), each with one preset input per space type and explicit windows (none on a wall shorter than 1 m or for a window below 0.3 m, ADR-011); *Transform Plan* to rotate and move a plan; four plan simplifiers (Z0–Z3); four floor aggregators (Stack, StackedFloorZoneMultiplier, SingleZonePerFloorType, SingleZoneBuilding) that designate floors and ceilings on the assembled stack and warn about unsupported storeys; invariant validation; and `Convert2BEM`, which writes heat transfer between zones only when asked (off by default). Wherever zones are merged and walls are rebuilt, every outdoor wall carries one centred window with the glazed area of the windows it covers, so glazing is conserved per wall, façade, orientation, and building (D-079); merged zones stay exact on rotated plans (D-088); every simplifier and aggregator supports footprints with holes, with the court façades as outdoor walls; and zone solids, surfaces, and windows are oriented out of their zones (D-091). Every component has an icon, and every component with a viewport preview has a *Preview Location* input that moves only its preview. The pipeline is described in [docs/architecture/pipeline.md](docs/architecture/pipeline.md); the example definition `examples/grasshopper/checkpoint-1-pipeline.gh`, which wires every simplifier into every aggregator, is still to be built by a person. Next: S8.5–S8.7 (the remaining four-source families, D-098), then Convert2IDF (S6) and Grasshopper UX and release (S9). The `Convert2BEM` output stays provisional until a ClimateStudio reference definition exists (D-023), and the example program presets hold illustrative values, not DOE prototype values.
```
