```markdown
### D-099 — BEMGen documents use the `SYN-TYP-*` family IDs

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (unsupervised run, D-098) · **Status:** Provisional
- Why: the precedent set is the four-source family set (D-093), whose IDs `SYN-TYP-001` to `SYN-TYP-019` cover every family; `SYN-TYP-001` to `SYN-TYP-009` carry the nine *High-Density Housing* families `HDHM-TYP-001` to `HDHM-TYP-009` with the same organisation and rule numbering.
- From S8.4 on, BEMGen documents name families by their `SYN-TYP-*` IDs and give the `HDHM-TYP-*` lineage for `001` to `009` ([syn-typologies.md](../research/precedents/syn-typologies.md)). `hdhm-typologies.md` stays as the issued baseline and is not updated further. Component descriptions and ADR-006 sections written in S8.1–S8.3 may keep their `HDHM-TYP-*` IDs as long as the `SYN-TYP-*` ID is also given; descriptions are updated when a component is next changed, and no GUID changes.
```

```markdown
### D-100 — The hub-and-arms families have at most four arms

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (unsupervised run, D-098) · **Status:** Provisional
- Why: the families of S8.5 that branch from a hub (`SYN-TYP-010`, `014`, `018`, `019`) share one layout, `HubArmsLayout`, which is axis-aligned in the plan frame of ADR-002, so its arms can only leave the hub on its four sides.
- The hub-and-arms families are limited to 3–4 arms, `018` to 2–4, as [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md) specifies: 3–4 lobes (`010`), 3–4 branches (`014`), 2–4 bedroom wings (`018`), and 2–3 halls (`019`, whose support wing takes one more arm, so 3–4 arms in all); a larger count is `InvalidParameter`. This is a limit of BEMGen's layout, not of the families, which set no bound (ADR-006: no family has an upper bound). It is revisited in S8.5 if a family needs a radial layout with arms at other angles.
```

```markdown
### D-101 — S8.4 complete

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (unsupervised run, D-098) · **Status:** Provisional
- Sub-stage S8.4 (D-098) is complete and tagged `v0.8.3`; after its gate and a fresh clone of the tag pass `scripts/verify.ps1`, `main` and `v0.8.3` are pushed (D-098). The four-source records of `typology-synthesizer` are pinned at `v0.3.1-research` (`7538ed3`) with their SHA-256 and paraphrased in `docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md` (D-093, D-099). The scope of D-094 is in the research brief (question 2, §4), the repository spec, README, AGENTS.md, and the roadmap (S8.4–S8.7 before S6; S9 after S6 and S8.7).
- [ADR-014](ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md), accepted by the controller under D-098 and pending the owner's reading, appends twelve `SpaceType` values (`Office`, `Retail`, `Mall`, `Kitchen`, `Dining`, `OperatingTheatre`, `CleanCorridor`, `DirtyCorridor`, `ClinicalSupport`, `CareBedroom`, `CareCommunal`, `ActivityHall`), reuses `Core`, `Lobby`, and `Service`, and gives each of the fifteen types one illustrative, conditioned preset in `ExampleNonResidentialPresets` (not DOE or standard values, D-023; ventilation only for the kitchen and the operating theatre; retail schedules with their own Saturday and Sunday) and a default preset component in *1 Program*, with icons. It also sets which rooms form one zone in every new family (D-097). ADR-006 specifies all nine new families (010, 011, 012, 014–019) with controller design defaults (D-098), the hub-and-arms families with at most four arms (D-100).
- New generators and components in *2 Generate*, with icons: *Office Plate* (`OfficePlateGenerator`, `SYN-TYP-017`, OPlate, `783cefaa-b836-4dbc-b16d-8e74820ed361`): an `Office` ring around a central `Core` zone without outdoor walls, at least 3 m of work area on every side. *Cafeteria* (`CafeteriaGenerator`, `SYN-TYP-016`, Cafe, `95956724-ddbc-48ce-9874-0ec6c244c8fe`): a `Kitchen` zone along the whole north edge of a larger `Dining` zone. The office ring is the first zone with a hole in a footprint without one; the surface builder, every simplifier, and every aggregator handle it without change. No tolerance or check was relaxed.
- The sixteen existing snapshots are byte-identical; two canonical snapshots are new. 193 core, 224 generator, and 704 integration tests pass on `net8.0`, including both families with every simplifier and aggregator on the canonical plan and on the plan rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1) m and rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m.
- Smoke test (D-056): the controller's scripted headless Grasshopper session on the verified reference (`f841f0c`, whose `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` equal the executed branch) in Rhino 8.25.25314.11001 (.NET 8.0.31) passed every performed item: the 15 preset and 2 generator components registered with their GUIDs and 24 × 24 icons; every preset with only its remark; the default office plate (1260 m², core solid of volume 410.4, office solid of 10 faces with 2 inner loops and volume 4377.6, walls toward the core facing into it) and cafeteria; all 96 combinations (both families, three placements, four simplifiers, four aggregators, 1/3/1) with *Validate* passing and no negative volume; and the error cases. It is recorded in `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md` and the close-out commit; the viewport look, the icons in the toolbar, and example definitions stay open for a person (D-058).
- S8.5 (families 010, 014, 018, 019 on the shared hub-and-arms layout) can start.
```

### Task 13: Fresh-clone gate and push (controller, D-098)

**Files:** none

D-098 pushes each sub-stage after its gate: the controller performs this task itself (a push is never delegated), and never with force. Its steps stay unticked in the plan, because they run after the close-out commit.

- [ ] **Step 1: Check the state to push**

```bash

git ls-remote --tags origin "v0.8.*"
```

Expected: no output from `git status --short`; the two SHAs are equal (the tag points at the tip of `main`); `fast-forward` (local `main` contains `origin/main`, which was at `2e3d3b1` when this plan was written); the tags `v0.8.0` to `v0.8.3`; `ls-remote` shows which of them the remote has. If `origin/main` has commits that local `main` does not, or the remote already has a `v0.8.3` that points at another commit, stop and report: nothing is pushed, and no rebase or force push is attempted. If the remote lacks one of `v0.8.0`, `v0.8.1`, or `v0.8.2`, report it to the person; this task pushes only `main` and `v0.8.3`.

## Exit criteria

- `docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md` paraphrases the 19 families of the pinned four-source records (`v0.3.1-research`, `7538ed3`, with the SHA-256 of the three record files), with lineage, support, and the BEMGen generator and sub-stage of each; `hdhm-typologies.md` names itself the issued baseline.
- ADR-014 records the new space types, the fifteen illustrative presets with their reasons, the default preset components, and the department zones of every new family (D-097); ADR-006 specifies all nine new families and records 017 and 016 as implemented with the pipeline consequences of S8.4.
- The research brief, the repository spec, README, AGENTS.md, and the roadmap cover building typologies of every program type (D-094), with S8.4–S8.7 before S6 and S9 after S6 and S8.7 (D-098); every relative link in `docs/`, `README.md`, and `AGENTS.md` resolves after Slice A (note 3).
- Twelve `SpaceType` values are appended after `Mixed`; `ExampleNonResidentialPresets` holds the fifteen presets with `SpaceTypes`, `ValuesOf`, and `PresetOf`, tested by `ExampleNonResidentialPresetsTests` (35 tests), which failed to build on the code before it.
- Fifteen default preset components in *1 Program* and two generator components in *2 Generate*, with the GUIDs of this plan, their defaults, required preset inputs, *Preview Location* last on the generators, and icons (53 icons, the existing ones byte-identical); no existing component, input, or GUID changed.
- `OfficePlateGenerator` (017, an office ring around a central core without outdoor walls, at least 3 m of work area on every side) and `CafeteriaGenerator` (016, a kitchen and servery along the whole north edge of a larger dining field) implement ADR-006 with their defaults, zone IDs, and validation; each has tiling, window, invalid-parameter, preset, determinism, provenance, and snapshot tests that failed to build on the code before them.
- Both families validate with every plan simplifier and floor aggregator, on their canonical plans and at two rotated and moved placements (`OfficePlateTypologyTests`, 51 cases; `CafeteriaTypologyTests`, 50 cases), with no `Lod.Core` change.
- Snapshots: the sixteen existing snapshots byte-identical; two new canonical snapshots.
- `pipeline.md`, `domain-model.md`, `convert2bem.md`, ADR-002, and `program-presets.md` describe S8.4; the S8.4 smoke-test checklist and its headless result are in the guide.
- `scripts/verify.ps1` prints `VERIFY PASSED`; tests: 193 core, 224 generators, 704 integration on `net8.0`; 0 warnings.
- Version 0.8.3, tag `v0.8.3`; AGENTS.md, README, the roadmap Progress line and detailed-plans row, and the decision-log entries D-099, D-100, and D-101 are written; this plan is ticked and marked Done.
- After a fresh clone of `v0.8.3` passes `scripts/verify.ps1`, `main` and `v0.8.3` are pushed without force (D-098).

## Notes for the reviewer

1. **Source.** Every code block, snapshot, and documentation replacement of Tasks 1 to 10 was extracted programmatically from a verified reference implementation built on `main` at `2e3d3b1`, 10 commits from `c3dd00b` to `f841f0c`, each passing `scripts/verify.ps1` with 0 warnings. The reference's commit order is kept with one exception: its ADR-014 commit (`e5ba806`, the third) is Task 2, before the scope alignment (`a0c4f97`, the second, Task 3), because AGENTS.md, the repository spec, and the roadmap link to ADR-014; the two commits touch disjoint files, so every file content in this plan is the content of the reference commit it comes from. The only content not taken from the reference is this plan's own commit, the three anchors of Task 3, Step 6 (note 3), and the close-out of Task 12 (adapted from the reference's proposed texts). Every task was then replayed on a worktree of `main` at `2e3d3b1` (its branch standing in for `main`; nothing fetched, pulled, tagged, or pushed), taking each file content and each replacement from this plan's text: the red build errors of Tasks 5, 7, and 8 (with line and column numbers and in the compiler's order), the single failing snapshot test of Tasks 7 and 8 and the received snapshots (byte-identical to the expected content), the failures of `make_icons.py` before Tasks 6 and 9 and the 51 and 53 hashes after them (every PNG byte-identical to the reference's), the counts after every task, a 0-warning build after every task, every documentation replacement (each "Replace" text occurs exactly once when it is applied), the link checks, the close-out edits, and `VERIFY PASSED` on every merge are observed results. The replay's end state equals the reference: `git diff --stat ref/s8.4 replay/s8.4` limited to the 47 paths the reference changes prints nothing for the state before the close-out, and after it lists only `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md`, and the roadmap, whose differences are exactly the close-out texts of Task 12; `Directory.Build.props` (0.8.3), the decision log, the plans, and the three anchors of Task 3 are files the reference does not change. Every observation of a first replay was observed again, unchanged, in a second replay of the final plan from a fresh worktree.
2. **Slices and branches.** The roadmap names no slices for S8.4; this plan uses four slices and a close-out, named after D-004: `docs/four-source-scope` (Tasks 1–4), `feature/non-residential-programs` (Tasks 5–6), `feature/office-plate-cafeteria` (Tasks 7–9), `docs/architecture-non-residential` (Tasks 10–11), and `chore/repo-s8.4-close-out`. Merges rebase onto local `main`. Unlike S8.1–S8.3, the stage is pushed at its end (D-098): Task 13 is the controller's, after the tag and a fresh clone of it pass `scripts/verify.ps1`. `origin/main` was at `2e3d3b1` when this plan was written, so the push is a fast-forward.
3. **Links.** The reference's scope commit renames the roadmap heading `### S8 — Typologies (S8.1–S8.3, D-083, D-084)` to `(S8.1–S8.7, D-083, D-084, D-098)`, which changes its anchor to `#s8--typologies-s81s87-d-083-d-084-d-098`; the header lines of the S8.1, S8.2, and S8.3 plans link to the old anchor and would no longer resolve (a link check over every Markdown file of the reference's end state found these three and nothing else). Task 3, Step 6 updates the three links; the reference does not, and these are the only changes to earlier plans. Putting ADR-014 (Task 2) before the scope alignment (Task 3) means that every link of Task 3 to ADR-014 resolves when it is committed. The precedent page and ADR-014 link to each other, so no order of two separate commits resolves both: between the commits of Task 1 and Task 2, the precedent page's two links to ADR-014 point at a file that Task 2 creates; the slice is merged only after both, so `main` never holds the unresolved link. A relative-link check (file targets and heading anchors, GitHub's slug rule) over every Markdown file of the replay found: no broken link at the plan commit or after any commit except Task 1's, whose two links from the precedent page to ADR-014 resolve with Task 2; after Tasks 2, 3, 4, 10, and 12, none.
4. **The baseline is 603 integration tests.** `5038512` (after `v0.8.2`, on `main`) made *Perimeter Core* reject self-intersecting offset rings and added `PerimeterCoreNeckTests` (two tests); D-092 records 601. S8.4 does not touch `Strategies.cs` or that test.
5. **Tests that were not test-first, and why.** The integration matrices of Tasks 7 and 8 (101 cases) passed at once in the reference and in the replay: the surface builder, the four simplifiers, the four aggregators, and validation already handle a zone with a hole inside a footprint without one (the office ring) and a plain two-zone rectangle, so no code change was needed and no red phase is possible; each task says to stop if a case fails. In the reference, an uncommitted sweep of 50 rotations from 0° to 357.7° with translations over the default office plate, an office plate at the 3 m minimum, and the default cafeteria ran 2400 simplifier and aggregator combinations without a failure; it was not replayed. Tasks 6 and 9 are Grasshopper code, checked by the build, the embedded icons, and Task 11. The build-error red phases list only declaration-level errors: the compiler stops before binding method bodies.
6. **`MinimumOfficeDepth`.** The 3 m minimum is a public constant of `OfficePlateGenerator`, not a `ToleranceSettings` value: it is a rule of the family (a core near a façade is outside `SYN-TYP-017`, and 3 m holds one row of desks with its aisle), like `WingCount ≥ 2`, and the depth is compared exactly (3 m is valid, as tested). D-087 (the *Perimeter Core* precondition) still applies downstream and is not imposed by the generator: the default plate passes it with the 4.57 m default depth.
7. **Department zoning and `SYN-TYP-017`.** The family speaks of several work areas around the core; D-097 makes the contiguous work area one department zone, a ring. ADR-006 records the tension and follows D-097. The cafeteria's servery counter and the customers' route along it are the shared edge between `KS` and `DI`, not zones (D-097, ADR-014).
8. **Upper bounds (D-100).** ADR-006 says that no family has an upper bound; the hub-and-arms families of S8.5 get a maximum (3–4 lobes or branches, 2–4 bedroom wings, 2–3 halls with a support wing), because the shared layout is axis-aligned. ADR-006 calls this a limit of the layout, not of the families, and the close-out records it as the provisional controller decision D-100, to be revisited in S8.5 if a radial layout is needed. It changes nothing in S8.4.
9. **Floor heights.** ADR-006's sentence that the floor height is 3 m for every family stays for the *High-Density Housing* families; the new families have their own (3.8 m office, 4 m cafeteria, 5 m mall, 6 m foyer and halls, 4.2 m operating suite, 3 m housing), stated in the new section.
10. **Rule IDs of `001`–`009`.** The precedent page and ADR-006 assume that the rule IDs of `SYN-TYP-001` to `009` equal the HDHM ones: the statements correspond one to one, but the records do not state the mapping.
11. **D-numbers.** The precedent page (Task 1) states the `SYN-TYP-*` ID rule as a provisional controller decision of S8.4 without a number, so that no number is committed before the controller assigns it; the close-out logs it as D-099, with D-100 (note 8) and the completion entry D-101.
12. **Smoke-test values.** The values of Task 11 were observed in one scripted headless Rhino 8.25 session (D-056) on the reference's last commit `f841f0c`, which reported the informational version `0.8.2+f841f0c…`; they were not re-observed on the replay. Task 11, Step 1 checks that `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` of the branch equal the reference's, so the record applies; the replay found no difference. The viewport, the toolbar icons, the *Inspect* texts beyond the footprint line, the zone IDs, the *Validate* glazing values, and *Convert2BEM* with Override = False were not observed.
13. **Presets.** `Schedule.FromDailyProfiles` supports one weekday and one weekend profile, so the retail schedules (their own Saturday and Sunday) are built by a private `Weekly` helper on `Schedule.Create`; no public API is added for it. The label and unit mapping of `DefaultPresetComponent` already covers gas equipment (W/m²) and ventilation (1/h), so no mapping is extended (the brief asked to extend it only if needed).
14. **Icons.** The 36 existing PNGs are byte-identical after Tasks 6 and 9 (Pillow 12.3.0); the new ones equal the reference's bytes. The contact-sheet descriptions are from the replay's sheets.
15. **Clean working tree.** The `git status` expectations assume a working tree with no untracked files outside `.gitignore` before Task 1.
16. **`check_plans.py` false positives.** The Global Constraints line that states the no-attribution rule is flagged as a possible attribution; it states the rule. The `make_icons.py` docstring line that names the Pillow version of the icons is flagged, as in S4.1 to S8.3.
